## cold-calling

### Cold Calling — Original Script

**Audience:** nsf · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** nsf, cold-call, young-adult

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 (Script A)**

> First start by SMSing them: "is this XXX?"
> 
> Then, give them a call:
> 
> Hello, is this XXX?
> Understand that you're currently serving NS?
> 
> XXX here from themoneybees. I'll just keep this call short, less than a minute. Basically we help young adults, including NSFs, save their money 60 times faster than the bank during their national service, and we meet many of them over their weekends.
> 
> So if you're interested to grow your savings faster, we can set a short session for you to find out more. Just to check, around where do you stay?
> 
> Okay, we will set a meeting sometime this/next weekend at (XXX mall) at 10am, and you can reply after this call to confirm the meeting, is that okay?
> 
> Alright so just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more, so as long as you come down and learn something beneficial for you, then that's good enough!

**Version 2 (Jamie's Script)**

> Text first: "Good morning/afternoon! is this xxx?"
> 
> **The CALL:**
> 
> Hi, [Name]! This is Jamie from themoneybees.
> Do you have a quick moment?
> 
> **If No:** When is a better time for me to call you back?
> 
> **If Yes:**
> I'll keep this call short, less than a minute.
> May I know if you are currently serving NS?
> 
> **If Yes (NS):**
> We are giving away a FREE adulting guidebook to help NSFs learn about saving, investing and other personal finance skills.
> Would this be something you'd be interested in receiving?
> 
> **If Interested:** Great! Would you mind me sharing more details about this with you over WA?

### Cold Calling — ORD Personnel (Agent Direct Call)

**Audience:** nsf · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** cold-call, nsf, ord

**Version 1 (Agent Direct Script)**

> Hi, Is this _____?
> 
> Hello _____, My name is ____, and I'm a financial planner for many ORD-Personels, I understand you have completed your NS right?
> 
> *(If Haven't Finished NS, check when and say we will call back in future)*
> 
> Are you currently studying at UNI or working? *(Fact find a little — what are they studying, which school, which year, etc)*
> 
> Alright, I'll keep this call short and let you know the purpose of the call. I'm calling because a few years ago, you indicated an interest in finding out more about ways to grow money faster than the bank. So, we currently have a free adulting and investing guidebook specially for ORD personnel. So if you're keen on receiving a copy of this book, there will be a fully online zoom session sometime this Saturday and Sunday 10am for us to go through, and afterwards we will be passing you a copy of the ebook.
> 
> *[Always give them a choice of Yes or Yes, don't ask open-ended questions]*
> 
> ## If Not Sure of Schedule
> 
> Tell them: Ok, how about we tentatively set a time first on [date and time] — if you have any changes, you can update us on WhatsApp.
> 
> **Example:** So would Wed 7pm work better for you?
> 
> *(If they genuinely sound like they cannot set a time, tell them no worries, we will call them back next time.)*
> 
> How about Friday 2pm or Saturday 10am? *[prompt one more time if no]*
> 
> ## After Agreeing on Time
> 
> Alright, what will happen next is that I'll drop you a WhatsApp text, and from there do acknowledge me through WhatsApp Ok? *(Pause 1 Second)*
> 
> And do make sure to come for this session where time slots are specially allocated for you as most ORD Personnel do find it beneficial for them after they have attended!
> 
> Alright then, see you this coming [date time]

### Cold Call — NSF Direct (In-Person Meet)

**Audience:** nsf · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** cold-call, nsf, in-person

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 (MoneyBees Script — V1 (Direct Meet))**

> Hello, understand that you're currently serving NS?
> 
> I'll just keep this call short less than 1 minute.
> 
> Basically we work with many NSFs to grow their savings **60 times faster** than the bank during NS. So if you're interested in growing your money faster than the bank during NS, we can have a meetup to go through more.
> 
> **Where is your nearest MRT?**
> 
> Alright, so I'll do a sharing session with you next Saturday 10am at [nearest mall], and after this call, I'll send you a WhatsApp, and all you have to do is to reply, is that okay?
> 
> So just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more, and as long as you learn something beneficial for you, then that's good enough.
> 
> Have a good day!

**Version 2 (MoneyBees Script — V2 (Campaign + Guidebook))**

> Hello, is this [Name]?
> 
> Understand that you're currently serving NS?
> 
> XXX here from TheMoneybees. I'll just keep this call short, less than a minute. So currently we are doing a **financial literacy campaign**, and just launched an **adulting guidebook** specially for young adults and NSFs.
> 
> Is it possible if I share with you more details through WhatsApp?
> 
> Basically, it will be a 30-min meeting to help you strategize on how we can help you save money better during NS, and we've done this for more than a few thousand young adults in the past few years.
> 
> After the session, we will also pass you a copy of our **free investment guidebook**.
>

> So just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more. As long as you learn something that's beneficial for you, then that's good enough! Just to check, how do I address you?
> 
> ---
> 
> > **Note:** Please continually wish them all the best for their book-in, book-out, build rapport. Even after the call, you still need to text them, wish them, etc.

**Version 3 (MoneyBees Script — V3 (60x + Mall Meet))**

> Hello, is this [Name]?
> 
> Understand that you're currently serving NS?
> 
> XXX here from TheMoneybees. I'll just keep this call short, less than a minute. Basically we help young adults, including NSFs, save their money **60 times faster** than the bank during their national service, and we meet many of them over their weekends. So if you're interested to grow your savings faster, we can set a short session for you to find out more.
> 
> **Just to check, around where do you stay?**
> 
> Okay, we will set a meeting sometime this/next weekend at [XXX mall] at 10am, and you can reply after this call to confirm the meeting, is that okay?
> 
> Alright so just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more, so as long as you come down and learn something beneficial for you, then that's good enough!

### Cold Call — NSF Telemarketer (Zoom Meet)

**Audience:** nsf · **Role:** telemarketer · **Tags:** cold-call, nsf, zoom

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 (Pamela's Script — V1 (Zoom + WhatsApp))**

> Hello (Name), I understand that you're currently serving NS? I'll just keep this call short, less than 1 minute.
> 
> Hi my name is Pamela from the Moneybees and we are a team of financial consultants who work with many NSFs to grow their NS allowance faster than the bank during NS. So if you're interested in learning how to grow your money and get started on investing, we can arrange for a consultant to have a meetup with you on Zoom to go through more and we can text you to let you know more.
> 
> ---
> 
> **If they respond: "Where did you get my number?"**
> 
> You did an ad on Instagram a while back.
> 
> > *Don't say what company you're from.*
> 
> **If they still say they don't remember:**
> 
> I am just a telemarketer for my manager. My manager reaches out to many people and some find his services very helpful and want to refer some of their friends to speak to us. I believe you are one of the contacts that he received, so I am calling in to check whether you would be keen to have a session with my manager.
> 
> ---
> 
> Would you be available next Saturday at 2pm? Great, after this call, one of the consultants will send you a WhatsApp, and all you have to do is to reply, is that okay?
> 
> ---
> 
> **If they respond: "I'm not sure if I'm free on that day. Can you send me more details and I get back to you once I confirm my schedule?"**
> 
> No worries. We can just set it the following weekend on [date] at 12pm on Zoom tentatively first. One of the consultants will send you some info through WhatsApp after this call, and you just help me to reply him to confirm the timing again okay?
> 
> ---
> 
> Alright, so just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more, and as long as you learn something beneficial for you, then that's good enough. Thank you and have a nice day!

**Version 2 (Pamela's Script — V2 (BMT / Savings Angle))**

> Hello, is this (Name)? I'm Pamela from MoneyBees. Just checking you're currently serving NS, right? I'll make this call very quickly.
> 
> We are a team of Financial consultants who help NSFs like you to grow your NS allowance faster than the bank — so your savings don't just sit there. Basically, we show simple and safe ways to make your money work harder — instead of just sitting in your savings.
> 
> If that sounds interesting, we can do a quick Zoom session where one of our consultants will explain how it works. I can send you the details on WhatsApp — is that okay?
> 
> ---
> 
> **If they ask: "Where did you get my number?"**
> 
> Oh! You actually interacted with an ad on Instagram a while back. I'm just a telemarketer helping my manager reach out to interested people. My manager speaks to a lot of NSFs, and many find the session really useful — some even refer their friends to join! I believe your contact came from one of those referrals or lists.
> 
> So I'm just calling to check if you'd be open to having a quick chat with him.
> 
> ---
> 
> Would Saturday at 2 PM work for you? If not, I can ask my consultant to send you more info on WhatsApp so you can confirm your schedule later — is that okay?
> 
> Great! Thank you, [Name]. It's just a learning session, so even if you pick up one or two helpful tips, that's already a good win. Have a great day!

### Warm Market — Conversation Openers (by Life Stage)

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** texting-eq, openers, warm-market, life-stage

*4 versions:*

**Version 1 (Investment / Returns Angle)**

> ### For friends who might be interested in investing:
> 
> > "Hmmm actually I want to show you how I've been doing investment planning for our [mutual group] friends especially now when the markets are CRAZY 😂 if we do the right things right, we can potentially earn good money over the next few years
> >
> > BUTTTT like that you say sometimes I also tell some people not to invest at all because they're not financially ready
> >
> > Catch you for 20 mins then you decide for yourself?? HEHEH"
> 
> ---
> 
> **Why it works:**
> - References mutual friends (common ground)
> - Shows excitement without being pushy
> - Safety valve: "sometimes I tell people NOT to invest"
> - Short time commitment: "20 mins"
> - Gives them authority to decide

**Version 2 (BTO / Couples Angle)**

> ### For friends who are getting a BTO or getting married:
> 
> > "Are ya'll gonna BTO?"
> > → leads to BTO planning and budgeting conversation
> 
> > "Hey! Since you're getting married soon — have you guys started looking at how to budget for the flat + wedding + insurance together? A lot of my clients in the same stage found it super helpful to map it all out."
> 
> ---
> 
> **Why it works:**
> - Ties into a real life event they're already thinking about
> - Positions you as helpful, not salesy
> - Natural lead-in to financial planning conversation

**Version 3 (Fresh Graduate Angle)**

> ### For friends who just graduated:
> 
> > "You graduated already?"
> > → "Have you finished your investment and insurance planning?"
> > → "Why not I show you what I do for my other clients!"
> 
> ---
> 
> **Why it works:**
> - Graduation is a natural transition point
> - They're likely thinking about adulting & finances
> - Low-pressure: "show you what I do" not "sell you something"

**Version 4 (Reprospecting / Second Attempt)**

> ### For friends who rejected you before:
> 
> > "You know a few years ago I reached out to you to talk about financial planning right?
> >
> > Actually I was a stupid noob back then who didn't know anything 😂
> >
> > So it's a good thing you rejected my request.
> >
> > BUT over the years especially these 2 years I've been planning for many couples to have a detailed plan towards financial freedom.
> >
> > After your wedding can I show you what I usually do for people? But no pressure."
> 
> ---
> 
> **Why it works:**
> - Self-deprecating humour disarms them
> - Acknowledges they were right to say no before
> - Shows growth and credibility
> - "No pressure" = safety valve

### Cold Calling — Voucher Leads (Young Adults)

**Audience:** young-adult · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** cold-call, voucher

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 (Gabriel's Script — Voucher Leads)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — Voucher Leads (Young Adults)
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Heyy, is this _____?
> >
> > *(Wait for response)*
> >
> > Heyy, this is Gabriel from **TheMoneyBees**. Recently you clicked on one of our Instagram ads about **[voucher name]**.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Value Prop
> 
> > Okay, our team has been helping **[fresh grads / NSFs / regulars]** get more clarity on investments and being able to **grow their money faster than the bank**.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Fact-Finding
> 
> > Have you started investing already?
> 
> **If No:**
> > Ah okay, no worries — that's actually very common. Can I ask what's been holding you back?
> >
> > *(Relate to them, build rapport, understand their pain point)*
> 
> **If Yes:**
> > Oh nice! What do you invest in? And how's it been going for you so far?
> >
> > *(Dig deeper — find gaps or frustrations to set your agenda)*
> 
> 💡 *The goal here is to uncover their pain point to set your agenda for the appointment.*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Setting the Appointment
> 
> > You free next **Sat 10am** for me to share some insights on investments and how to **build up your wealth from scratch** — even without a lot of money?
> >
> > Because there's a misconception that in order to become rich or build up your wealth, you need to earn like **$10k a month** — but you actually don't, if you have the **right strategies**.
> 
> **If Yes:** Great, let me set that on the calendar! 📅
> 
> **If No:** No worries — check availability and **set tentatively**.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Closing & WhatsApp Confirmation
> 
> > Okay, I'll send you a **WhatsApp message** — then you just help me to reply to confirm, ah?
> >
> > See you, bye! 👋

**Version 2 (Kenny's Script — Yakun Voucher Leads)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script V2 — Yakun Voucher Leads
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hey **[Name]**!
> >
> > Hi, I'm **Kenny** from **Moneybees**. You've recently clicked onto our Instagram ads and done a survey to redeem some **Yakun vouchers** — do you remember?
> >
> > *(Wait for reply)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### The Hook — Zoom + Guidebook
> 
> > Hey great! I'll help you redeem it through a quick **20–30 min Zoom session** where I'll share more insights on **investments** and some ways where you can **grow your money faster than the bank**.
> >
> > After the session, I'll actually pass you our **investment guidebook** by our community. How would that sound?
> >
> > *(Wait for reply and adapt)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Fact-Finding
> 
> > Have you started your own investing yourself already?
> >
> > *(Aim at their pain points — dig into why/why not, what they invest in, frustrations, goals)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Setting the Appointment
> 
> > Would **Wed 3pm** or **Fri 5pm** be good for a Zoom session?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Closing
> 
> > Great! I'll just text you on **WhatsApp** with this number and you reply to me, okay? 👍

### Cold Calling — Instagram Ad Lead (Young Adults / NSF)

**Audience:** young-adult · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** cold-call, instagram-ad

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 (Gabriel's Script — Ads V1 (Structured))**

> Hey good evening, is this [Name]? How's your day today?
> 
> This is Gabriel from TheMoneyBees and I'm calling because you recently clicked on one of our Instagram ads about growing your money. Do you remember?
> 
> This call will only take 3 minutes of your time, can I check if that's okay for you? *(pause)*
> 
> ---
> 
> Over the years, our team has helped many NSFs and regulars with their financial planning as well as investments — because these are things that you typically don't learn in school.
> 
> I wanted to check on your availability sometime this week for a quick Zoom for me to share some insights regarding:
> 
> • Proper financial planning
> • How to properly allocate your NS allowance
> • How to build up your wealth from scratch
> 
> Of course I understand that army can be busy at times — that's why the sharing will be held over Zoom for about 30 minutes to make things more convenient for you!
> 
> Would Saturday or Sunday at 7pm work for you?
> 
> *(Alternative: How about Saturday/Sunday when you book out?)*

**Version 2 (Gabriel's Script — Ads V2 (Conversational))**

> Heyy this is Gabriel from TheMoneyBees, recently you clicked on one of our Instagram ads about how to grow your money and investments. Do you remember?
> 
> Okay, our team has been helping NSFs and regulars get more clarity on investments and being able to grow their money faster than the bank.
> 
> Have you started investing already?
> 
> ---
> 
> **If No:**
> 
> Ah okay, no worries — that's actually very common. Can I ask what's been holding you back? *(Relate to their situation, build rapport, understand their pain point)*
> 
> **If Yes:**
> 
> Oh nice! What do you invest in? And how's it been going for you so far? *(Dig deeper — understand their experience, find gaps or frustrations to set your agenda)*
> 
> ---
> 
> **Setting the appointment:**
> 
> You free next Sat 10am for me to share some insights on investments and how to build up your wealth from scratch — even without a lot of money?
> 
> Because there's a misconception that in order to become rich or build up your wealth, you need to earn like $10k a month — but you actually don't, if you have the right strategies.
> 
> ---
> 
> **If Yes:** Great, let me set that on the calendar! 📅
> 
> **If No:** No worries, when are you free then? Let me set it tentatively first.
> 
> ---
> 
> Okay, I'll send you a WhatsApp message — then you just help me to reply to confirm, ah? 👍

### Cold Call — Facebook Ads (Ebook Angle)

**Audience:** young-adult · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** facebook-ad, ebook, cold-call, young-adult

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 (MoneyBees Script)**

> Hello [Name]!
> 
> We're from the @moneybeesacademy! [www.instagram.com/moneybeesacademy](https://www.instagram.com/moneybeesacademy)
> 
> I think you might have seen our Instagram Ads recently regarding the pre-launch of a financial book that we're giving away for the **first 100 young adults**.
> 
> Anyway, you should be quite young right?
> 
> I mostly speak to young adults about better financial planning and investing!
> 
> The reason why I'm doing this is that I have gone through the entire education system in Singapore and they actually didn't really teach anything much about personal finance, and hence I was pretty frustrated about this, and wrote a book:
> 
> 📘 [The Ultimate Guide to Adulting](https://leotanfinancial.sg/the-ultimate-guide-to-adulting/)
> 
> It aims to empower and educate more young people about investing and financial literacy! I conduct workshops too :)

**Version 2 (Jamie's Script — Cold Lead (Adulting Guidebook))**

> **Text first:**
> > Good morning/afternoon! Is this [NAME]?
> 
> ---
> 
> ## The Call
> 
> Hi, [NAME]! This is Jamie from The Moneybees.
> 
> Do you have a quick moment?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### ❌ If No:
> 
> No problem — when is a better time for me to call you back?

> 
> ---
>

> 
> I'll keep this call short, less than a minute.
> 
> May I know if you are currently serving NS?
> 
> ---
> 
> #### 🪖 If YES (Serving NS):
> 
> That's great, because we are actually a financial education platform for NSFs and young working adults.
> 
> And the reason I am calling is because we are giving away a **FREE adulting guidebook** to help NSFs like yourself learn more about saving, investing, and other personal finance skills.
> 
> Would this be something you'd be interested in receiving?
> 
> **✅ If Yes:**
> > Great! Would you mind me sharing more details about this with you over WhatsApp?
> >
> > *— OR —*
> >
> > I will send you more details about this over WA and we can chat there, is that alright with you?
> 
> Thank you so much for your time and have a great day!
> 
> **❌ If No:**
> > No worries / That's ok, I understand.
> >
> > *(If I could ask you real quick — is it a timing issue or something else?)*
> 
> Thank you so much for your time and have a great day!
> 
> ---
> 
> #### 🎓 If NOT Serving NS:
> 
> Ok, are you currently studying or working now?
> 
> The reason I am calling is because we are currently conducting a financial literacy campaign and just launched a **FREE adulting guidebook** for young working adults and students.
> 
> Would this be something you'd be interested in receiving?

> > *— OR —*
> >
> > I will send you more details about this over WA and we can chat there, is that alright with you?
> 
> Thank you so much for your time and have a great day!
> 
> **❌ If No:**
> > No worries / That's ok, I understand.
> >
> > *(If I could ask you real quick — is it a timing issue or something else?)*
> 
> Thank you so much for your time and have a great day!

### Cold Calling -- Working Adults (All Angles)

**Audience:** working-adult · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** ebook, passive-income, qualified-lead, facebook-ad, cold-call, text-message, cpf

*8 versions:*

**Version 1 (Kenny's Script)**

> Hi XXX, I understand that you're currently studying or working now?
> 
> Okay I'll just keep this call short, less than a minute.
> 
> I'm Kenny from MoneyBees.
> 
> We are currently having a financial literacy campaign and we are giving away a free adulting guidebook specially for young adults, because we don't learn stuff like how to manage your money, budgeting and CPF in school.
> 
> Can I send you more details on WhatsApp?
> 
> And, on top of that, we can also do a quick zoom session with you this Saturday at 10am, and I'll be going through with you our adulting guidebook.
> 
> Okay great, I'll send you a text after this call to confirm, and all you have to do is just to reply my WhatsApp, is that okay?
> 
> So just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more, and as long as you learn something, then that's good enough.
> 
> Just to check, how do I address you? (if no name).
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 📝 Notes
> 
> - Please continually wish them all the best for their book in, book out, build rapport
> - Even after the call, you still need to text them, wish them, etc.
> - Also, our consultant will give a quick call later on to run through the materials. Will sometime later at 5/6pm be good?

**Version 2 (Short Script — Ebook + WhatsApp Permission)**

> Hello, is this [Name]?
> 
> XXX here from TheMoneybees.
> 
> We are currently having a **financial literacy campaign** and we are giving away a **free adulting guidebook** specially for young adults.
> 
> Is it possible if I send you more details through WhatsApp?
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 💡 Tips
> 
> - **Important:** Emphasize giving away the **free adulting book** and sharing more details on WhatsApp so we can gain permission to send them more details.
> - These are **super cold leads** — they have never heard of us before. Because they don't know where we are from, it's hard to set appointments on the call.
> - By sending details and a **link to our Instagram**, they will be able to see that we are actually professional and have credibility, so it's **easier to set appointments from there**.

**Version 3 (Telemarketer Script — Investing + Free Course)**

> Heyy this is [Name] from the MoneyBees, a financial education platform.
> 
> I understand that you're currently studying or working now?
> 
> *(Give them time to reply.)*
> 
> Our team has been helping many young adults grow their money faster than the bank, and we've compiled everything into a **free online course**.
> 
> And just to check, have you started investing already?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### If No:
> 
> *(Relate to them and ask why)*
> 
> Alright, so our consultant will share some insights on investments and how to **build up your wealth from scratch** even without a lot of money or time to do your own investments.
> 
> ### If Yes:
> 
> *(Ask what they invest in and how it's going for them)*
> 
> Our consultant will share some insights on **optimising your investments**.
> 
> ---
> 
> Will sometime later at **5/6pm** be good for a quick callback by our consultant?
> 
> Okay great, so as long as you learn something from this, then that's good enough.
> 
> Thank you so much for your time and have a great day!
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 📝 Notes
> 
> - If they have no name: **"By the way, how do I address you?"** → then place their name in the CRM
> - If they ask **"Where did you get my number?"**: Reply: *"You signed up for a campaign some time back, you probably forgot about it."*

**Version 4 (MoneyBees Script)**

> Hello! Is this [Name]?
> 
> I'm [Name] from TheMoneybees!
> 
> I'll keep this call short, less than 1 minute! Understand that you recently took part in a **[campaign]**?
> 
> This is a courtesy call to inform you that we have received your interest in participating in this campaign! To qualify, we will need to schedule a short **20–30 minute session**, where I will do a **financial health review** for you and send you a **report** afterwards.
> 
> Would you prefer to meet on weekdays or weekends?
> 
> ---
> 
> **After they reply:**
> 
> Alright great, so I will schedule the session tentatively for this **Saturday 10am**. After this call, I will send you a text message, and all you have to do is to reply and acknowledge — is that okay?
> 
> Alright great. So just one last thing, this session is just for you to find out more, and as long as you learn something, then that's good enough!

**Version 5 (Family Financial Planning Specialist V1)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — FB Family Financial Planning Leads
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hey, is this **[Name]**?
> >
> > We've received your interest via our **Facebook ad on family financial planning** — not sure if you recall that?
> >
> > Are you available now for a few mins quick chat?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Personal Introduction + Value Proposition
> 
> > I'm a **family financial planning specialist**, who is also a **mummy of two young kids**. I work very closely with many parents to help them optimize their **children's education fund** and **retirement setup** — both on the income side and the expense side. For example:
> >
> > ✅ Creating additional streams of **passive income** to fund their retirement or **children's education**
> >
> > ✅ Reviewing existing **insurance or retirement policies**
> >
> > ✅ Minimizing **unnecessary costs** that tend to creep up as we age
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Fact-Finding (Family & Education Fund)
> 
> > Currently are you married and how many kids do you have? How old are they?
> >
> > Have you started saving for their **education fund**?
> >
> > - **If yes:** How are you saving it — via bank or investment?
> > - **If no:** What's holding you back from doing that?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Insight Hook — Conservative Parents
> 
> > Throughout our **20+ years of experience** in working with parents like yourself, we realized that many parents are afraid to lose money — hence have been very conservative when it comes to accumulating wealth.
> >
> > So we have curated some **insights and strategies** to help parents **reduce their investment risks** and at the same time **grow their money at a faster rate**.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Zoom Session Pitch
> 
> > Normally we do this over a **Zoom call**, where we show you different **options and variations** so you can see what's most suitable for your situation.
> >
> > At the very least, it's an **information-sharing session**. Worst-case, you walk away with the **NTUC voucher**. Best-case, you get insights that meaningfully improve your retirement strategy.
> >
> > Think of it like **window-shopping** before you buy a pair of shoes — you just want to explore options, compare, and see what fits you best.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Booking
> 
> > Not sure — would you prefer to have this Zoom call on a **weekday or weekend**? **Morning or afternoon**?
> 
> ---
> 
> 💡 *Confirm the time slot and send a WhatsApp message to lock in the appointment.*

**Version 6 (Golden Year Partners — Voucher Lead V1)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — FB Voucher Leads (Working Adults)
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hi, is this **[Name]**?
> >
> > I'm **[Your Name]**, a Retirement Specialist from **Golden Year Partners**. This is a courtesy call to inform you that we have received your interest in redeeming some **[voucher name]** vouchers recently! Is it a convenient time to speak now?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Value Proposition
> 
> > So I'll just keep this call short — I'm a retirement specialist and I help many people around your age to **retire earlier and faster**, by:
> >
> > ✅ Increasing your **passive income streams**
> > ✅ Reducing your **expenses**
> >
> > We specialise in helping Singaporeans create a **simple and secure passive income**, and discover ways to supplement their **CPF and other assets**, ensuring they're on track to meet their retirement goals.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Policy Optimisation
> 
> > And I believe you should have purchased many policies over the years, but these policies may not be effective for your retirement needs.
> >
> > So, we can help you **optimise some of them** and use these resources to create **additional streams of passive income**.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Soft Close
> 
> > The **worst case** is that this is an info session to get more clarity and insights about your situation — and the **best case** is that we can do something to actually **improve your situation**.
> >
> > Would sometime **this or next weekend** work better for you for a meetup?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Objection Handling
> 
> | Objection | Response |
> |---|---|
> | **"No time"** | Perhaps we can have a chat over a phone call — would **[date/time]** work to run through this with you? |
> | **"Not sure of schedule"** | Can I set a tentative timing, perhaps at **[date/time]**? Just to check — where is your nearest MRT? |
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Booking Confirmation
> 
> > Alright, for now I'll tentatively place it on **[date/time]** at **[nearest mall/café]**, to run through your retirement planning.
> >
> > So just one last thing — this session is just for you to learn more about growing a **simple and secure passive income**, and as long as you learn something, then that's good enough!
> 
> ---
> 
> ### If Asked "What's This About?"
> 
> > It's a **retirement planning consultation**. You can also read more here:
> >
> > 🔗 https://consult.goldenyearpartners.com/

### Cold Calling — CPF Retirement Changes

**Audience:** pre-retiree · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** cpf, retirement

**Version 1 (CPF Pre-Retiree Script)**

> ## Greetings
> 
> Hi good evening, is this xxx? I am XXX calling on behalf of xxx.
> 
> We are reaching out to you because we understand that you are over 55 this year am I right?
> 
> ## Purpose
> 
> ANW, The PURPOSE of this call, is to share with you 2 important changes mentioned in Budget 2024 last year that affect your CPF Retirement Account after you turn 55. This impacts your retirement and legacy that many people do not know.
> 
> Do you have a min? Let me give you a QUICK one
> 
> The first change that REALLY affect most Singaporean is that SA account will be closed for all age 55 and above which has already implemented.
> 
> A lot of Singaporean used to collect the 4% interest from SA yearly, However, SA will be shut down and all of your CPF savings are forced to earn 2.5% in the OA which is even lower than the inflation rate.
> 
> SECONDLY based on Budget 2024, alot of Singaporeans don't realise that interest generated in CPF Life will not be part of their legacy, in fact this interest is more than $150,000 for the majority that hit FRS. This amount will be returned to CPF pool instead of your loved ones in an event of legacy.
> 
> ## Leading to Closing
> 
> From these two changes, those who are most affected are people Working for more than 20-30 Years as they would have hit the FRS of $205,800
> 
> Sir / Mdm, can I check if you fall in this majority group? Because 8 out of 10 Singaporeans hit FRS before age 55.
> 
> ## If Yes
> 
> Sir / Mdm, many at your age group have benefitted from meeting our specialist, can I arrange an appointment for you. Basically, He will be going through 3 important items that will affect your CPF Retirement Account.
> 
> It will only take 20 min of your time, at least you will be able to make a more informed decision.
> 
> Would you prefer to meet during weekday or weekend?

---

### Cold Calling — Facebook Ad Opt-In (CPF eBook)

**Audience:** pre-retiree · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** cpf, ebook, facebook-ad

**Version 1 (Retiresmart Script)**

> Hello, is this [Lead's Name]?
> 
> Hi [Lead's Name], I'm Xenia/Angeli, calling from Retiresmart, I'll just keep this call short.
> 
> This is just to confirm that we've received your interest to get a free copy of our CPF & retirement planning guides recently and we'll be passing you a copy of them later on!
> 
> Can my retirement specialist give you a quick 5min call later on today to pass you some of the materials and also to explain the recent CPF life changes?
> 
> ## Qualifying Question
> 
> Are you still looking for more information on retirement planning?
> 
> **If Yes:**
> That's great! Let's go into more details.
> 
> **If No:**
> I see, no worries. Just to clarify, are you above 55?
> 
> Regardless of age, how has your retirement planning been going so far?
> 
> ## Building Rapport
> 
> Many of our clients in a similar situation have found our resources very useful in navigating their retirement planning. Would you be keen to hear more about how we can help?
> 
> ## Introducing Common Concerns
> 
> Most of the people we speak to fall into one of these two groups:
> 
> **Group 1:** Those concerned about the SA account closure for those aged 55 and above, looking for ways to grow their savings beyond the 2.5% p.a. in the OA.
> 
> **Group 2:** Those considering the upcoming increase in the ERS to 4X of the BRS, wanting to know if topping up is worthwhile or exploring alternative options.
> 
> Do either of these apply to you?
> 
> ## Engaging Based on Their Response
> 
> (Engage in conversation based on their specific concerns.)
> 
> ## For Leads Within 2 Months
> 
> That's great, (Lead)! As a thank-you for opting into our resources, you're entitled to a complimentary 30-minute Zoom session with one of our retirement specialists.
> 
> They'll assess your current retirement readiness and explore ways to optimise your CPF and savings for a better retirement. Would that be helpful for you?
> 
> **If they say yes:**
> 
> So after this call, I'll just send over the resource guide, and get my retirement specialist to drop you a call to schedule the session. Would that be okay?
> 
> Is [time] a good time for you later for a quick call?

> ### Objection Handling
> 
> <table style="min-width: 50px;"><colgroup><col style="min-width: 25px;"><col style="min-width: 25px;"></colgroup><tbody><tr><th colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p>Objection</p></th><th colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p>Response</p></th></tr><tr><td colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p><strong>"No time"</strong></p></td><td colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p>Perhaps we can have a chat over a phone call — would <strong>[date/time]</strong> work to run through this with you?</p></td></tr><tr><td colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p><strong>"Not sure of schedule"</strong></p></td><td colspan="1" rowspan="1"><p>Can I set a tentative timing, perhaps at <strong>[date/time]</strong>? Just to check — where is your nearest MRT?</p></td></tr></tbody></table>
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Booking Confirmation
> 
> > Alright, for now I'll tentatively place it on **\[date/time\]** at **\[nearest mall/café\]**, to run through your retirement planning.
> > >
> > So just one last thing — this session is just for you to learn more about growing a **simple and secure passive income**, and as long as you learn something, then that's good enough!
> > >
> 
> ---
> 
> ### If Asked "What's This About?"
> 
> > It's a **retirement planning consultation** — we will share more details in the WhatsApp. You can also read more here:
> > >
> > 🔗 [https://consult.goldenyearpartners.com/](https://consult.goldenyearpartners.com/)
> > >

### Cold Calling — Telemarketer Script for Voucher Leads (Parents)

**Audience:** parent · **Role:** telemarketer · **Tags:** cold-call, voucher, family

**Version 1 (Telemarketer Script — Voucher Freebie Campaign (Parents))**

> Hi, is this (name)?
> 
> I'm [name], from the moneybees. This is a courtesy call to inform you that we have received your interest in redeeming some [name of vouchers] vouchers recently!
> 
> We might have called previously, but you were busy.
> 
> In order to redeem it, we will need to schedule a quick callback with one of our financial consultants.
> 
> We help many young parents in Singapore to grow their money faster than the bank, and plan better for their family.
> 
> So if you're interested in growing and protecting your money better, can my consultant give you a callback later at around [XX] timing to share more about it?
> 
> Okay great, after this call, my manager will send you a text message, and all you have to do, is to reply and acknowledge, is that okay?
> 
> Alright great. So just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more about planning better for your money and your family, and as long as you learn something, then that's good enough!

### Telemarketer -- Young Adults / NSF (All Lead Types)

**Audience:** young-adult · **Role:** telemarketer · **Tags:** voucher, cold-call, text-message, zoom

**Version 1 (Telemarketer Script — Voucher Freebie Campaign)**

> Hi, is this (name)?
> 
> I'll just keep this call short less than 1 minute!
> 
> Understand that you're currently studying or serving your national service?
> 
> I'm [name] from themoneybees!
> 
> This is a courtesy call to inform you that we have received your interest in redeeming some [name of vouchers] vouchers recently!
> 
> So I believe we have texted you previously about it! In order to redeem it, we will need to schedule a short 20minute zoom session, whereby one of our consultants will share more about growing your money and investing. After the call, you will get an investing guidebook and also the vouchers.
> 
> **Would you prefer to call weekends or weekdays?**
> 
> [wait for reply]
> 
> Alright great, so I will schedule the session tentatively for this saturday 10am and after this call, my manager will send you a text message, and all you have to do, is to reply and acknowledge, is that okay?
> 
> Alright great. so just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more about growing your money, and as long as you learn something, then that's good enough!

### Telemarketer — Cold Leads (Multi-Version)

**Audience:** cold-lead · **Role:** telemarketer · **Tags:** cold-call, text-message, hospital-plan, free-course

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 (Version 1: Changes in Hospital Plan Rider)**

> Hi, understand that you're currently studying or working now?
> 
> Okay, I'll just keep this call is just for less than a minute.
> 
> Its regarding the recent changes in the hospital plan rider. We may have called you previously, but I think you were quite busy.
> 
> So, its important to understand these changes as it will affect how much you can claim for any hospital bills.
> 
> Is it okay if my manager calls you back later on to share with you more information?
> 
> Will sometime later at 5/6pm be good?
> 
> Thank you so much for your time and have a great day!
> 
> ---
> 
> **If they have no name:**
> "By the way, how do I address you?" → then place their name in the CRM

**Version 2 (Version 2: Free Online Course)**

> Hi, are you currently studying or working?
> 
> No worries — I'll keep this really quick, less than a minute.
> 
> My name is Pamela from MoneyBees. We work with many young adults to help them strengthen their financial planning, especially since these things aren't really covered in school. After speaking with a lot of people from your age group, we put together a free online course that shows simple ways to grow your money more effectively.
> 
> I understand you're busy, so I'll send the full details to you on WhatsApp — would that be alright?
> 
> Our consultant will also give you a quick call to walk you through the materials. Would [insert date/time] be good for you?
> 
> Great! As long as you gain something useful from this, that's already a win.
> 
> Thank you for your time, and I hope you have a wonderful day!

### Telemarketer — Golden Year Partners (All Lead Types)

**Audience:** pre-retiree · **Role:** telemarketer · **Tags:** cold-call, cpf, qualified-lead, mass-affluent, retirement

*4 versions:*

**Version 1 (Template (Telemarketer — Facebook Voucher Leads (Golden Year Partners)))**

> Hi, is this (name)?
> 
> I'm [name], from Golden Year Partners. This is a courtesy call to inform you that we have received your interest in redeeming some [name of vouchers] vouchers recently!
> 
> We might have called previously, but you were busy.
> 
> ............................................
> 
> In order to redeem it, we will need to schedule a meetup with one of our retirement specialists.
> 
> We specialise in helping Singaporeans create a simple and secure passive income, and we've helped many individuals discover ways to maximize their CPF and other assets, ensuring they're on track to meet their retirement goals.
> 
> Would you be opposed in exploring how our expert advisors can help you create a personalized plan?
> 
> Can my consultant give you a callback later at around [XX] timing to share more about it?
> 
> Okay great, after this call, my manager will send you a text message, and all you have to do, is to reply and acknowledge, is that okay?
> 
> Alright great. So just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more about growing a simple and secure passive income, and as long as you learn something, then that's good enough!

**Version 2 (Template (Telemarketer — Mass Affluent Working Adults (Golden Year Partners)))**

> Hi, is this (name)?
> 
> I'm [name], from Golden Year Partners. We might have called you before but you were busy. Very quickly, we specialise in helping Singaporeans create a simple and secure passive income, and we've helped many individuals discover ways to maximize their CPF and other assets, ensuring they're on track to meet their retirement goals.
> 
> So if you would like a personalised retirement plan, can my consultant give you a callback later at around [XX] timing to share more about it?
> 
> Okay great, after this call, my manager will send you a text message, and all you have to do, is to reply and acknowledge, is that okay?
> 
> Alright great. So just one last thing, this session is just for you to learn more about growing a simple and secure passive income, and as long as you learn something, then that's good enough!
> 
> ---
> 
> **If asked what's this about:**
> Retirement planning consultation, we will share more details in the WhatsApp and my consultant will also give you a call later. You can also read https://consult.goldenyearpartners.com/ if needed for more information.

**Version 3 (Template (Telemarketer — Qualified Leads CPF Angle (Golden Year Partners)))**

> Hi, is this (name) speaking?
> 
> Hi (name), this is just (your name) calling from Golden Year Partners.
> 
> I'm the admin assistant for our retirement specialists.
> 
> I saw you indicated interest in our retirement planning & CPF resources a while back via our online ads, just want to check if you're looking out for information with regards to retirement planning?
> 
> **(yes)** → proceed to next
> 
> **(no)** I see, may I check if you're past the age of 55 years old?
> 
> Doesn't matter if yes or no → I see, how is your retirement planning journey going so far? (chit chat)
> 
> Actually many of our readers who (same profile as the prospect) have found our resources and sessions to be quite useful to help them better plan towards their retirement, if you don't mind I can share with you a bit more?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Two Common Categories
> 
> Usually people who request for our resources, they usually fall into one of these two categories. Maybe I'll just share them with you, see if you resonate with any of them:
> 
> 1. **CPF SA Closing** — With CPF SA account will be closing next year for those 55 and above, and they are wondering if there are better ways to grow their money above the 2.5% p.a. in OA
> 
> 2. **ERS Increasing** — With ERS increasing to 4X of BRS next year, some are considering if it's worth doing the top up, or if there are better options out there, so they want to make a more informed decision.
> 
> Would you say any of these resonate with you?
> 
> (first, second, or whatever reason they share, chit chat with them)
> 
> ---
> 
> ### For leads within 2 months:
> 
> That's great (name), usually for most of our readers who share this concern, what I'd recommend is for me to arrange a short 30 minute zoom session for one of my retirement specialists to share with you more about possible options which you can explore, to help you plan towards a better retirement.
> 
> Would you prefer weekdays or weekends?
> 
> (proceed to set appointment)
> 
> ---
> 
> ### For leads more than 2 months:
> 
> That's great (name), as recently there have been some changes in the retirement planning landscape and we want to make sure you guys get the latest information. So what we've come up with is a short PDF guide based on the recent CPF changes and how you can best plan towards your retirement. Would you like me to drop you a copy via WhatsApp later?
> 
> **(okay sure / yes)**
> 
> Great, I will send it to you right after we end this call. This guide will definitely be useful based on what you've shared with me. However, it may not help you to get full clarity on your retirement. We do have clients in the similar situation being able to achieve their retirement goals, or even having more than what they're planning for.
> 
> Regardless if you're interested in becoming our clients, what our consultants can share with you is what are some of the frameworks and methods our clients are using to help them achieve an additional $1,000 to $2,000/month in passive income, would that be something you'd be open to?
> 
> (proceed to set appointment)
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Once appointment set:
> 
> Okay so what's gonna happen next is I will be sending you a confirmation text and a brief agenda of what we will be running through during the session so you can have a clear idea of what to expect. I will also send you a reminder one day before the allocated time slot. Do me a favour and help me acknowledge the text, is that okay?
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 📌 Calling Tips
> 
> - **Take note** the calling script only serves as a guide. When it comes to calling, a lot of it comes to how you interact with the respective leads.
> 
> - **Be friendly and cheerful** in your tonality. The more likeable you are, the more people are willing to chat.
> 
> - **Sell the value of appointment and the retirement specialists** — e.g. "Many people who have scheduled a consultation session with us have found it to be very useful, as our retirement specialists focus on creating a tailored strategy that will really benefit you and help you work towards your ideal retirement."
> 
> - **Prompt responses** — When the leads come in during daytime (9am to 6pm), contact them within a 20 minute window, so they remember that they have opted in for something.

**Version 4 (Derek Tan)**

> Hi, good afternoon, is this (Name)?
> 
> I’m [Name] from Golden Year Partners. This is a courtesy call as we received your interest in redeeming a Yakun voucher from one of our recent online campaigns.
> 
> To redeem the voucher, we’ll just need to schedule a short Zoom session with one of our retirement specialists.
> 
> During the session, the advisor will share how he has helped many pre-retirees create a simple and secure passive income, and also how some individuals are maximising their CPF and other assets to stay on track for retirement.
> 
> Would you be open to finding out how our advisor can help you create a personalised plan? Can my advisor give you a call back later around (XX timing) to share more about it?
> 
> Alright, great. I would also like to share that this session is mainly for you to learn more about creating a simple and secure passive income, and I’m sure you’ll gain a better understanding of your retirement options after the call.
> 
> Thank you for your time today, and have a great day ahead!

### Cold Calling -- Recruitment (All Lead Sources)

**Audience:** recruitment · **Role:** telemarketer · **Tags:** recruitment, facebook-ad, masterclass, text-message, nsf, zoom, job-portal, webinar

*8 versions:*

**Version 1 (Catalyst Recruitment — NSF Script)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — NSF Leads (Recruitment)
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hi, is this _____?
> >
> > Hello _____, my name is ____, I'm a recruitment officer from Catalyst Recruitment. I understand that you are going to **ORD this year**?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### The Pitch
> 
> > Great — I'll keep this call short. I'm calling because we currently have **internship opportunities specifically for NSFs** after they complete their NS, and I wanted to check if this would be relevant for you.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Fact-Finding
> 
> > Just to confirm, are you planning to go to **university after NS**?
> 
> *(If yes, continue below)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Booking the Session
> 
> > Alright, what we usually do is arrange a short sharing session to walk you through the opportunities. This would be a **group briefing** for you to learn more about the various job opportunities we currently have.
> >
> > How about we tentatively set a time first, and you can update us later if anything changes via WhatsApp.
> >
> > Would **Thursday at 5:30pm** work better for you, or **Friday at 2:00pm**?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Closing
> 
> > What will happen next is that I'll send you a **WhatsApp message** shortly. Once you receive it, please acknowledge me on WhatsApp, okay?
> >
> > Also, do make sure to attend this session as **time slots are specially allocated**, and many university-bound students find it very beneficial after attending.
> >
> > Have a great day ahead! 👋

**Version 2 (Catalyst Recruitment — NSF Script V2 (Detailed))**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — NSF Leads V2 (Recruitment)
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hi, is this _____?
> >
> > Hello _____, my name is ____, I'm a recruitment officer from Catalyst Recruitment. I understand that you are going to **ORD this year**?
> >
> > Great — I'll keep this call short.
> 
> ⚠️ *Always give them a choice of Yes or Yes — don't ask open-ended questions.*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Fact-Finding
> 
> > By the way, just to check — are you going to **university** after NS?
> 
> ⚠️ *If they are going to Poly or ITE → please drop the lead.*
> 
> ⚠️ *Insert whether they are going to Uni/Poly in the **Telemarketer Remarks** section.*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### If They're Unsure of Their Schedule
> 
> > OK, how about we tentatively set a time first on **[date and time]** — if you have any changes, you can update us on WhatsApp.
> >
> > So would **Thursday 5:30pm** work better for you?
> 
> *(If they genuinely can't commit: "No worries, we will call you back next time.")*
> 
> > How about **Friday 2pm** or **Saturday 10am**?
> 
> *(Prompt one more time if no)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### After Agreeing on a Time
> 
> > Alright! What will happen next is that I'll drop you a **WhatsApp text**, and from there do acknowledge me through WhatsApp, OK?
> >
> > *(Pause 1 second)*
> >
> > And do make sure to come for this session where time slots are specially allocated for you — most **uni students** do find it beneficial for them after they have attended!
> >
> > Alright then, see you this coming **[date and time]**! 👋

**Version 3 (NSF Script V3 — Free Course & Side Hustle)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — NSF Leads V3 (Free Course Pitch)
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hi!
> >
> > Understand that you have **completed your national service** already?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Quick Pitch
> 
> > Okay, I'll just keep this call short — **less than one minute**.
> >
> > We currently have a **free online course** to help students and young adults learn more about **high income skills** and planning for their future.
> >
> > If you're interested in **growing your income** and exploring **side hustle opportunities**, I can send you more details to find out more — is that okay?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Consultant Follow-Up
> 
> > Also, my consultant will also be **calling you later at around 5pm** to share with you more details!
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Closing
> 
> > Just to check, how do I address you?
> >
> > So just one last thing — as long as you **learn something new** from this, then that's good enough!
> >
> > Have a nice day! 👋

**Version 4 (FINternship Masterclass Script)**

> ## 📱 Facebook Ads Texting Script — Masterclass/Webinar
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hi, is this **[First Name]**?
> >
> > Hi [First Name], I'm **[Your Name]** and have recently received your interest in our **Financial IQ Masterclass** to learn how you can earn $10k/month. Can I check if I got the right person?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### If Yes — Invite to Masterclass
> 
> > Okay great! Our next Financial IQ Masterclass will be happening next month on the **21st of June**. Would you be keen/free to attend?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### ✅ If They Say YES
> 
> > Great! I will send you our reservation link in a bit. Once you have registered, do drop me a text.
> >
> > Do take note that we also have an introduction course over Zoom on **Wednesdays 7pm**, **Fridays 2pm**, and **Saturdays 10am**. Do let me know if you'd be able to attend!
> 
> **Send as text:**
> 
> > Hi [Name], do go ahead to register here:
> >
> > 🔗 https://tinyurl.com/finternshipeventbrite
> >
> > Do let me know once it's done. The tickets are **fully refundable upon attendance**.
> >
> > 📅 **Date:** 21st June (Saturday)
> >
> > 🕜 **Time:** 1:30 PM (Registration starts)
> >
> > 📍 **Venue:** Singapore Shopping Centre, #02-20A, S239924
> >
> > 👉 Beside Dhoby Ghaut MRT — opposite The Cathay
> >
> > 👕 **Dress Code:** Smart Casual (no shorts)
> >
> > Just reply **"YES"** so I can send over important event info and reminders leading up to the masterclass.
> >
> > Best regards,
> >
> > **[Your Name]**
> >
> > FINternship
> 
> ---
> 
> ### ❌ If They Say NO
> 
> > Okay no worries! We have alternatives — Zoom sessions available weekly on **Wednesdays at 7pm**, **Fridays at 2pm**, and **Saturdays at 10am**.
> >
> > I'll drop you the information via text. Do let me know which slot would be most convenient for you!
> 
> ---
>

>

> >
> > No worries if you can't make it! Before our masterclass, we will conduct a **personalised Zoom financial strategy session** with our consultant **[Name]**, who has experience speaking to hundreds of young adults.
> >
> > This is what you will be getting:
> >
> > 🔥 Access to hours of materials from the world's best minds in our community
> >
> > 🧠 Learn the money language schools never teach
> >
> > 🌟 Strategies to fast-track your wealth, passively
> >
> > 📚 Bonus resources to kickstart your journey to earn your first **$200K pot of gold** a decade earlier than the rest
> >
> > Some bonus resources after the session!
> >
> > *[Insert the books]*
> >
> > I've scheduled your session for **[Confirmed Date & Time A]**, or **[Confirmed Date & Time B]** — will any of these timings work for you?
> >
> > Do reply **"A"** or **"B"** to indicate which time you prefer.
> 
> ⚠️ *Same SOP as before — remind 3 days before, 1 day before, and on the day itself.*
>

**Version 6 (Catalyst Recruitment Script)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — Recruitment
> 
> **Opening:**
> 
> > Hi, is this _____?
> >
> > Hello _____, my name is ____, I'm a recruitment officer from Catalyst Recruitment. I understand that you may be looking out for career or internship opportunities at the moment?
> 
> *(If not looking, ask: "How about opportunities in the future?")*
> 
> ---
> 
> **Fact-Finding:**
> 
> > Are you currently studying at uni or working?
> 
> *(Find out: what they're studying, which school, which year, or where they're working now)*
> 
> ---
> 
> **The Pitch:**
> 
> > Alright, I'll keep this call short. I'm calling because currently we have some job and internship opportunities for young adults that are **flexible and also work from home!**
> 
> *(If already working: "It's suitable for mid-career switchers also.")*
> 
> ---
> 
> **Booking the Interview:**
> 
> > So if you're keen to find out more, we are currently conducting **group interviews**. Will either **Thursday 5:30pm**, **Friday 2pm**, or **Saturday 10am** work for you?
> >
> > There will be a total of 3 rounds of interviews, so this will just be the first round and it will take 30 mins.
> 
> ⚠️ *Always give them a choice of Yes or Yes — don't ask open-ended questions.*
> 
> ---
> 
> **If they're unsure of their schedule:**
> 
> > No worries! How about we tentatively set a time first on **[date and time]** — if you have any changes, you can update us on WhatsApp.
> >
> > So would **Thursday 5:30pm** work better for you?
> 
> *(If they genuinely can't commit: "No worries, we will call you back next time.")*
> 
> > How about **Friday 2pm** or **Saturday 10am**?
> 
> *(Prompt one more time if no)*
> 
> ---
> 
> **After agreeing on a time:**
> 
> > Alright! What will happen next is that I'll drop you a **WhatsApp text**, and from there do acknowledge me through WhatsApp, OK?
> >
> > *(Pause 1 second)*
> >
> > And do make sure to come for this session where time slots are specially allocated for you — most uni students do find it beneficial for them after they have attended!
> >
> > Alright then, see you this coming **[date and time]**! 👋

**Version 7 (Catalyst Recruitment — Job Portal Script)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — Job Portal Leads (Recruitment)
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hi, is this _____?
> >
> > Hello _____, I'm calling from **Catalyst Recruitment**. Understand that you recently applied for a job on **[insert name of job portal]** for a **[insert name of job role]**.
> >
> > Just to inform you that you've been **shortlisted for an interview** — would you be available for a **group briefing** this or next week? There are a total of **4 rounds of interviews**, and this will be the first round.
> >
> > Will you be free this **Thursday 5pm**, **Friday 2pm**, or **Saturday 10am**? It's a short **30-minute Zoom call**.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### If Asked 'What's the Role About?'
> 
> > It's a **WFH position** with **flexible working hours** in the **[name of role]**. More details will be shared in the job briefing, and this is just the first of 4 rounds.
> 
> ⚠️ *Always give them a choice of Yes or Yes — don't ask open-ended questions.*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### If They're Not Sure of Their Schedule
> 
> > OK, how about we **tentatively set a time** first on **[date and time]** — if you have any changes, you can update us on WhatsApp?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### If They Want More Details
> 
> > It's a position in a **[name of job role]**. If you're interested, would you like me to send over a text message and schedule a **group interview** for you?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### After Agreeing on a Time
> 
> > Alright! What will happen next is that I'll drop you a **WhatsApp text**, and from there do acknowledge me through WhatsApp, OK?
> >
> > *(Pause 1 second)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ## 🛡️ Objection Handling
> 
> ### 1. 'Not interested'
> 
> > I understand! No worries, as this is just a **group briefing** and there is a total of **4 rounds of interviews**. So there's no harm to go and find out more.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 2. 'What is the session about?'
> 
> > This would be a **group briefing** for you to learn more about the position you applied for.
> >
> > So would **[date and time]** work better for you?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 3. 'Is this a sharing session? Or a 1:1 session? Do you have any link to your organisation?'
> 
> > The job role is under **Catalyst Consultancy**.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 4. 'How did you get my number?'
> 
> > I'm not sure as it's in my system, but you might have placed your details on one of the **job portals**.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 5. 'Which organisation are you calling from?'
> 
> > I'm actually calling from **Catalyst Recruitment** — we are a recruitment agency.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 6. 'What is your name, want to complain'
> 
> ⚠️ *At most give your **first name**. Do NOT give your contact or mobile number.*
> 
> > Hi [Name], I understand your frustration. However, this is just a service call to check if you are keen to understand how to better explore your internship and career options. I apologise for the inconvenience caused, if any.
> 
> ⚠️ *Then **HANG UP** — the more you talk, the more they will try to find trouble.*
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 📅 Available Timings
> 
> - **Thursday** — 5:00 PM
> - **Friday** — 2:00 PM
> - **Saturday** — 10:00 AM

**Version 8 (Catalyst Recruitment — FB Leads Webinar Script)**

> ## 📞 Cold Calling Script — Facebook Leads (Recruitment Webinar)
> 
> ### Opening
> 
> > Hi, is this **[First Name]**?
> >
> > Hi **[First Name]**, I'm **[YOUR NAME]** from **Catalyst Recruitment**, and to keep this really quick — we have recently received your interest in attending our **free seminar**!
> 
> ---
> 
> ### The Pitch
> 
> > It's about helping people learn how to **build additional income streams**.
> >
> > It's **not a motivational talk** — it's a **30-minute Zoom call** where we'll cover:
> >
> > - What is a **franchise business** about
> > - How the speaker personally built a **7-figure business as a fresh graduate**
> > - How to **get started** even with **limited time/capital**
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Booking
> 
> > Will you be available on either this **Thursday 5pm**, **Friday 2pm**, or **Saturday 10am** for the Zoom session?
> >
> > There will be a total of **3 rounds of interviews** after the session if you're shortlisted after the first session.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Closing & WhatsApp Confirmation
> 
> > Alright, I'll send you a message on **WhatsApp** — please **acknowledge me** once you receive it, okay?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 📅 Available Timings
> 
> - **Thursday** — 5:00 PM
> - **Friday** — 2:00 PM
> - **Saturday** — 10:00 AM

## prospecting

### Warm Outreach – Direct Approach (Existing Clients)

**Audience:** clients · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** warm-market, direct, existing-client, investment, whatsapp, jana-prospecting

**Version 1 — Direct Approach — Existing Client (Jana) (Jana (Auctus))**

> # Warm Outreach – Direct Approach
> **Best for:** Existing clients you already have a relationship with
> 
> ---
> 
> ## The Script
> 
> Hi [Name]! Hope you are having a great start to [Year]!
> 
> I know you mentioned that you want to focus on [their current savings/investment plan]. Just checking if you would be open to having a quick look at our new [product/fund] as a possible option to diversify?
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Why This Works
> 
> - Opens with genuine warmth and a personal touch
> - References a previous conversation to show you listen and remember
> - Frames it as an option, not a hard sell
> - The word diversify appeals to financially-aware clients
> - Low-pressure: just checking if you would be open gives them an easy out
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Key Principles
> 
> - **Recall their goals** — mention what they told you last time
> - **One product at a time** — keep the ask focused
> - **Give them control** — suggest Zoom or a meeting time that suits them
> - **No pressure language** — avoid words like must, should, or urgency tactics
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Tips from Jana
> 
> > For existing clients, I go straight to the point but always reference something they shared with me before. It shows I was paying attention and makes the outreach feel personal, not transactional.

---

### Warm Outreach – Soft Approach (Warm Market / Community Contacts)

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** warm-market, soft, community, whatsapp, non-pushy, jana-prospecting

**Version 1 — Soft Approach — Warm Market / Community (Jana) (Jana (Auctus))**

> # Warm Outreach – Soft Approach
> **Best for:** Friends, community members, church mates, social circle — people you know but have not spoken to professionally
> 
> ---
> 
> ## The Script
> 
> Hi [Name]! Hope all is well on your end. :)
> 
> I just thought of asking — are you currently working with any financial consultant for your planning needs?
> 
> If not, or if you are open to exploring, I would love to see if we can work together. I have been working with some of our [community/group] friends for years now. They have entrusted me with their financial planning and I am truly blessed to be part of their journey.
> 
> No pressure at all — I just thought of reaching out to see if you would be open for a quick sharing. :)
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Why This Works
> 
> - Starts with a question, not a pitch — far less intimidating
> - Asking if they currently work with anyone is non-threatening and opens dialogue naturally
> - Social proof via community builds credibility through shared identity
> - No pressure at all explicitly removes the awkwardness of being sold to
> - Ends with a light, open invitation — not a close
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Key Principles
> 
> - **Lead with a question** — let them volunteer their current situation
> - **Social proof from shared community** — mention mutual friends or group members (with permission)
> - **Never assume they need help** — frame it as exploration
> - **One ask only** — just ask if they are open, do not jump to scheduling in message 1
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Tips from Jana
> 
> > For warm market contacts — especially community or church friends — I never lead with a product. I lead with curiosity about their situation. The moment they say they do not have anyone yet or they are open to exploring, that is your green light.

---

### Warm Outreach – Curiosity Approach (DIY Investors / Existing FA Contacts)

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** warm-market, curiosity, diy-investor, portfolio-review, no-pressure, jana-prospecting

**Version 1 — Curiosity Approach — DIY Investors / Existing FA Contacts (Jana) (Jana (Auctus))**

> # Warm Outreach – Curiosity Approach
> **Best for:** Contacts who are financially savvy, already self-managing investments, or have an existing FA
> 
> ---
> 
> ## The Script
> 
> I get you, and I honestly respect that you are very hands-on with your finances. That shows how intentional you are with your financial planning — especially with insurance, tax-saving plans, and dividend investing.
> 
> Do you have someone helping you with all of these? :)
> 
> What I usually do in situations like this is a one-time portfolio assessment — just to spot any blind spots, overlaps, or things that might be worth a second look. No harm in checking.
> 
> If it sounds useful, we can have a chill chat over coffee or tea. Up to you. :)
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Why This Works
> 
> - Leads with a genuine compliment — validates their effort without making them feel inadequate
> - Reframes the ask — not a sales pitch, but an offer to check if everything is optimised
> - Blind spots is a powerful word — it implies there could be gaps without being insulting
> - Low commitment ask — a chill chat over tea feels very different from a formal consultation
> - Up to you returns control to them, reducing resistance
> 
> ---
> 
> ## When to Use This
> 
> - Contact mentions they already have an FA
> - Contact seems financially independent or handles their own investments
> - Contact brushes off financial conversations as already sorted
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Key Principles
> 
> - **Never compete with their current FA** — position yourself as a second opinion, not a replacement
> - **Portfolio assessment framing** — one-time, low-pressure, no commitment
> - **Casual setting** — suggest tea, coffee, or a quick Zoom, never a formal presentation
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Tips from Jana
> 
> > When someone says they already have an FA or manage their own investments, that is not a dead end. I offer a one-time portfolio review — not to replace anyone, just to give them a fresh set of eyes. Most people are open to a second opinion when you take the pressure off.

---

### Warm Outreach – Event Follow-Up & Nurture (Lifestyle Prospecting)

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** warm-market, event-follow-up, lifestyle-prospecting, door-gift, nurture, jana-prospecting

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 — Event Follow-Up — Missed the Event (Jana) (Jana (Auctus))**

> # Event Follow-Up – Missed the Event
> **Best for:** Contacts you invited to a lifestyle event (fitness class, lunch, gathering) who could not make it
> 
> ---
> 
> ## The Script
> 
> Hi [Name]! Hope you are feeling much better now!
> 
> I am sorry you could not make it to the [event name] — we missed you there!
> 
> I actually had a personalised door gift prepared for you, so I would still love to pass it to you. Let me know when it is convenient and I can meet you at [location]!
> 
> Also, while we catch up, I can briefly share about a new [product/fund] that was recently launched — thought it might be something you would find interesting as well. Let me know if this week or next works better for you. :)
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Why This Works
> 
> - The door gift is a brilliant non-salesy reason to meet in person — creates genuine goodwill
> - Briefly share softens the business pitch — it is secondary to the catch-up
> - Giving two time options makes it easy to say yes
> - The entire message feels like care, not selling
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Key Principles
> 
> - **Events create warmth** — always follow up within 48 hours
> - **The gift creates reciprocity** — it opens doors without being transactional
> - **Business is secondary** — lead with the relationship and the gift, not the product
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Jana Prospecting System
> 
> > I attend one social event per month to meet new people. I also run lifestyle events like fitness classes that allow natural, non-transactional connections. The door gift strategy is key — it gives me a genuine reason to follow up with people who could not make it.
> 
> **Weekly schedule:**
> - Monday and Friday: Dedicated prospecting blocks
> - Daily: Small consistent outreach habit
> - One Saturday per month: Social events or gatherings
> - Quarterly: COI (Centre of Influence) meet-ups

**Version 2 — Nurture Follow-Up — Long-Lost Contact (Jana) (Jana (Auctus))**

> # Nurture Follow-Up – Reconnecting with Old Contacts
> **Best for:** Contacts you have not spoken to in a while who reach out or resurface
> 
> ---
> 
> ## The Script
> 
> Hi [Name]! Happy New Year! It has been forever — hope you are well!
> 
> *(Wait for their reply. If they bring up finances or life goals, respond warmly:)*
> 
> Thank you for trusting me with this. That is actually a great mindset already — wanting to be wiser with your finances.
> 
> [Amount] is a good starting point, and we can definitely explore options that fit your goals and comfort level.
> 
> Would you be able to meet for a consultation sometime next week? Let me know which day works and we will take it from there. :)
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Why This Works
> 
> - Responding warmly to their initiative removes all pressure — you are just following their lead
> - Starting point framing validates small amounts and makes it feel achievable, not embarrassing
> - Asking about a specific day creates momentum without being pushy
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Key Principles
> 
> - **Do not pitch first** — let them bring up the topic
> - **Validate any amount** — never make someone feel their budget is too small
> - **Keep it light** — this is a consultation, not a close
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Tips from Jana
> 
> > The best leads often come from people who reach out to you. Stay warm and consistent with your network, and when they are ready, they will find you.

---

## warm-market

### Market Survey Script

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** market-survey, recruitment, internship, appointment-setting, objection-handling

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 — Version 1 – Still Thinking of Joining (AIA)**

> **For Candidates still thinking of joining the Career**
> 
> # MARKET SURVEY 1 (5 minutes)
> 
> **-Introduce yourself, build rapport, update about yourself and the internship**
> **-Ask for help to complete a Market Survey**
> 
> 1. Do you have any insurance policies / financial plans? (Yes/No)
> 
>    If no, why? _______________________________________________
> 
> 2. When was the last time you did a review of your policies?
> 
>    _______________________________________________
> 
> 3. How do you feel about your existing consultant's service?
> 
>    _______________________________________________
> 
> 4. I am currently interning with AIA and I might consider financial services as a career. If I choose to do so, I like to extend my services to my closest friends. You are one of those whom I love to work with. Will there be a possibility of me meeting you for a Financial Health Review if I am successfully hired? (Yes / No?)
> 
>    _______________________________________________

**Version 2 — Version 2 – Already Intending to Join (AIA)**

> **For Candidates already intending to join the Career**
> 
> # MARKET SURVEY 2 (5 minutes)
> 
> **-Introduce yourself, build rapport, update about yourself and your new career**
> **-Ask for help to complete a Market Survey**
> 
> 1. Do you have any insurance policies / financial plans? (Yes/No)
> 
>    If no, why? _______________________________________________
> 
> 2. When was the last time you did a review of your policies?
> 
>    _______________________________________________
> 
> 3. How do you feel about your existing consultant's service?
> 
>    _______________________________________________
> 
> 4. As you may be aware, I am undergoing training to become a licensed financial advisor. As part of my training, I am tasked to conduct Financial Health Reviews. Will it be alright if I can meet you for a 30-min session to share with you the work that I do? Relate the Promises: Will not force, Future Meetings.. (Yes / No?)
> 
>    _______________________________________________

**Version 3 — Full Call Script (Opening → Objections) (AIA)**

> # Market Survey Call Script
> 
> > **Minimum 30 to be completed before Application Phase**
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Opening
> 
> Hello, good morning, is this John?
> 
> John, this is.... Do you have a moment to speak?
> 
> How are you this period? *(Rapport Build)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Agenda
> 
> As you may have learnt, I am *"intending to embark on the career of a Financial Advisor"* / *"currently interning with a Financial Services Agency"* and I am tasked to do a simple market survey with the people that I know. Would you be able to help me by just giving me 5 minutes of your precious time?
> 
> *(Proceed with Market Survey Q1, Q2, Q3)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Setting the Appointment *(Using Question 4)*
> 
> Thanks XXX. By the way, *"when I am officially licensed to start my practice"* / *"let's say if I decide to embark on the practice to become a Financial Advisor"*, one of my priorities is to extend my service to my closest friends. You are one of those whom I highly regard.
> 
> Till then, may I get in touch with you for a 30-minute session to share with you on the type of solutions I can offer? You have my assurance that during our meeting, I will not ask you to do anything you don't want to and any future meetings will depend entirely on whether you feel my services would be of help to you. Is that fair?
> 
> Good. Would it be best to get together at your office or your home or through Zoom? That will be great! Would weekdays or weekends be better for you? Would office hours or evenings be better for you? Could I see you on XXXday at XX, or would XXXday at XX be better?
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Closing Off
> 
> Really appreciate your help. Will it be alright if I can keep you posted of any AIA promotions that is relevant to you? May I have your email address and date of birth so I can connect better with you?
> 
> *(Optional: Last but not least, may I have your permission to add you on Instagram or FB so we can keep each other updated on our happenings?)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ## Objection Handling
> 
> **"Not Interested"**
> > Mr...., I can understand you're not being interested in something you haven't had an opportunity to see but, so that you can judge for yourself, would you be free for a short time on...
> 
> **"Not in the Market"**
> > Mr...., I would have been surprised if you had said you were in the market for life insurance right now. However, I do have some ideas that will be handy for you when you're ready to go ahead. Would you be free...
> 
> **"No Money"**
> > Mr...., I can understand you trying to hold down unnecessary expenses. However, I do think you'd find what I have to say of interest and, of course, you'd be under no obligation. Would you be free....
> 
> **"No Need"**
> > Of course, Mr..., you'd be the sole judge of whether this particular idea would be of value to you. Since it will take only a short time for me to explain it to you, would you be free...
> 
> **"Too Busy"**
> > Mr...., I guessed that you'd be busy and that's why I telephoned for an appointment rather than call on the off-chance of seeing you. Would you be free...
> 
> **"Wasting Your Time"**
> > Mr...., on the chance that you'll see value in the idea, I'd be happy to spend the time with you. Would you be free for a short time on...
> 
> **"What's the Idea?"**
> > Would you be free... Mr...., to explain it properly, I will need to show you some illustrations and discuss them personally with you.
> 
> **"Is it Insurance?"**
> > It's the protection of your family / a savings plan with difference etc. that I would like to talk to you about. With some illustrations that I'm sure you'll find interesting, I can explain it to you in a brief interview. Would you be free...
> 
> **"Post it Out"**
> > I'd be happy to do that Mr..., but what I have in mind will be useful only if it's tailored to your individual needs. That's why I'd like to see you in person. Would you be free...
> 
> **"For the Obstinate Objector"**
> > Well, Mr..., could we leave it this way: I'd like to meet you and if I'm in your neighbourhood during the next few months, I'd like to drop in and say "hullo". If you have a few moments at that time, I'll be glad to show you what I have in mind.
