### Initial Text — Facebook Leads (Recruitment)

**Audience:** recruitment · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** recruitment, facebook-ad, text-message, initial-text

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 (Text 1 — Verification)**

> ## 📲 Initial Text — Facebook Leads for Recruitment
> 
> ### First Text (Verification)
> 
> > Hey **[Name]**, received your interest recently for our **free seminar** 🎉
> >
> > Just to check if I got the right person before I send more details?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 📎 Attach These Flyers
> 
> ![Hit Your First $10K](/scripts/hit-first-10k.webp)
> 
> ![Free Seminar — Sign Up Now](/scripts/free-seminar-signup.webp)
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 💡 Notes
> 
> - Send this as the **first touchpoint** before calling
> - Wait for them to confirm identity before sending full details
> - If they reply "yes" → follow up with **Text 2** (seminar details + Zoom booking)
> - If no reply after 24h → proceed with a **cold call**

**Version 2 (Text 2 — Seminar Details + Zoom Booking)**

> ## 📲 Text 2 — Seminar Details & Zoom Booking
> 
> *Send this after they confirm identity from Text 1*
> 
> ---
> 
> > Hey **[Name]**,
> >
> > This is about a **free seminar** where we teach motivated people how to **build additional income streams**.
> >

### Callback Call — Pre-Retiree Consultation (Telemarketer Set)

**Audience:** pre-retiree · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** callback, cpf

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 (Consultant Callback Script — Shoe Analogy V1)**

> ## 📞 Callback Script — Pre-Retiree Consultation
> 
> *Use when the telemarketer has already set a callback appointment with the prospects*
> 
> ---
> 
> **Opening:**
> 
> > Hey, is this **\[Name\]**?
> > >
> > 
> > Great. This is regarding the **retirement planning consultation**. I believe my assistant spoke with you yesterday to arrange this call. I tried giving you a ring earlier, but you might've been tied up, so let me just give a quick introduction.
> > >
> 
> ---
> 
> **Value Proposition:**
> 
> > I'm a **retirement specialist**, and I work closely with many pre-retirees to help them optimize their retirement setup — both on the **income side** and the **expense side**. For example:
> > >
> > 
> > ✅ Creating additional streams of **passive income**
> > >
> > 
> > ✅ Reviewing existing **insurance or retirement policies**
> > >
> > 
> > ✅ Minimizing **unnecessary costs** that tend to creep up as we age
> > >
> 
> ---
> 
> **Fact-Finding:**
> 
> > Have you started any retirement planning so far?
> > >
> > 
> > Are you currently working?
> > >
> > 
> > Do you already have some existing investment policies that can help fund your retirement?
> > >
> 
> ---
> 
> **Policy Optimisation Pitch:**
> 
> > Many people I meet have **older insurance policies** they bought years ago that no longer match their needs. Hospital plans also get **more expensive with age** — sometimes hitting the 4- or even 5-figure range yearly.
> > >
> > 
> > What we do is help you **optimize all of this**. In the right scenario, instead of paying hundreds of thousands to insurers over your lifetime, we can restructure things so these costs become an **asset for you** — potentially even getting you a hospital plan at **no additional cost** and creating **passive income** for retirement.
> > >
> 
> ---
> 
> **Soft Close (Shoe Analogy):**
> 
> > At the very least, it's an **information-sharing session**. Worst-case, you walk away with the Yakun voucher. Best-case, you get insights that meaningfully improve your retirement strategy.
> > >
> > 
> > Think of it like **window-shopping** before you buy a pair of shoes — you just want to explore options, compare, and see what fits you best.
> > >
> > 
> > That's what this consultation is about: showing you the different **options and variations** so you can see what's most suitable for your situation.
> > >
> 
> ---
> 
> **Booking:**
> 
> > For this kind of conversation, it works best to **meet in person**. Which area do you stay around?
> > >
> 
> ---
> 
> 💡 *Set the meeting at the nearest mall café and confirm via WhatsApp.*

**Version 2 (中文版 + 汉语拼音 (Chinese + Pinyin))**

> ## 📞 回电话脚本 — 退休规划咨询（中文 + 拼音）
> 
> *电话推销员已安排回电后使用*
> 
> ---
> 
> **开场白：**
> 
> > 喂，您好，请问是 **[名字]** 吗？
> > *Wéi, nín hǎo, qǐngwèn shì [míngzi] ma?*
> >
> > 你好，我这通电话是关于**退休规划咨询**的。
> > *Nǐ hǎo, wǒ zhè tōng diànhuà shì guānyú tuìxiū guīhuà zīxún de.*
> >
> > 我助理昨天应该有先和您联系，安排今天的通话。
> > *Wǒ zhùlǐ zuótiān yīnggāi yǒu xiān hé nín liánxì, ānpái jīntiān de tōnghuà.*
> >
> > 我刚才也有试着给您打电话，可能您当时比较忙，所以我先做一个简单的自我介绍。
> > *Wǒ gāngcái yě yǒu shìzhe gěi nín dǎ diànhuà, kěnéng nín dāngshí bǐjiào máng, suǒyǐ wǒ xiān zuò yīgè jiǎndān de zìwǒ jièshào.*
> 
> ---
> 
> **关于专业介绍：**
> 
> > 我是退休规划的**专业顾问**，主要是协助即将退休或正在准备退休的客户，帮助他们优化整体的退休方案，包括：
> > *Wǒ shì tuìxiū guīhuà de zhuānyè gùwèn, zhǔyào shì xiézhù jíjiāng tuìxiū huò zhèngzài zhǔnbèi tuìxiū de kèhù, bāngzhù tāmen yōuhuà zhěngtǐ de tuìxiū fāng'àn, bāokuò:*
> >
> > ✅ 提升退休时期的**被动收入**
> > *Tíshēng tuìxiū shíqí de bèidòng shōurù*
> >
> > ✅ 检视现有的**保险或退休相关保单**
> > *Jiǎnshì xiànyǒu de bǎoxiǎn huò tuìxiū xiāngguān bǎodān*
> >
> > ✅ 减少随着年龄增长而不断上升的**不必要开销**
> > *Jiǎnshǎo suízhe niánlíng zēngzhǎng ér búduàn shàngshēng de bù bìyào kāixiāo*
> 
> ---
> 
> **关于常见问题（保单优化）：**
> 
> > 很多人之前买的旧保单，可能已经不太符合现在的需求了；医疗住院计划随着年龄增长也会越来越贵，有些人甚至每年要付到**四位数甚至五位数**的保费。
> > *Hěnduō rén zhīqián mǎi de jiù bǎodān, kěnéng yǐjīng bútài fúhé xiànzài de xūqiú le; yīliáo zhùyuàn jìhuà suízhe niánlíng zēngzhǎng yě huì yuèláiyuè guì, yǒuxiē rén shènzhì měinián yào fù dào sì wèi shù shènzhì wǔ wèi shù de bǎofèi.*
> >
> > 我们会协助您做**整体优化**。
> > *Wǒmen huì xiézhù nín zuò zhěngtǐ yōuhuà.*
> >
> > 在适合的情况下，原本可能要在一生中付给保险公司的几十万，我们可以帮您把它转换成属于您的**资产**，让您拥有**免费的住院计划**，同时还能为您的退休带来额外的**被动收入**。
> > *Zài shìhé de qíngkuàng xià, yuánběn kěnéng yào zài yīshēng zhōng fù gěi bǎoxiǎn gōngsī de jǐ shí wàn, wǒmen kěyǐ bāng nín bǎ tā zhuǎnhuàn chéng shǔyú nín de zīchǎn, ràng nín yōngyǒu miǎnfèi de zhùyuàn jìhuà, tóngshí hái néng wèi nín de tuìxiū dàilái éwài de bèidòng shōurù.*
> 
> ---
> 
> **关于会面的目的（鞋子比喻）：**
> 
> > 最差的情况，就是一次**资讯分享**；您也一样能拿到那张**亚坤礼券**。
> > *Zuì chà de qíngkuàng, jiù shì yīcì zīxùn fēnxiǎng; nín yě yíyàng néng ná dào nà zhāng Yàkūn lǐquàn.*
> >
> > 最好的情况，是您能获得真正对自己有帮助的资讯，**改善您的退休规划**。
> > *Zuì hǎo de qíngkuàng, shì nín néng huòdé zhēnzhèng duì zìjǐ yǒu bāngzhù de zīxùn, gǎishàn nín de tuìxiū guīhuà.*
> >
> > 就像买鞋子前会先逛一逛一样，看看哪一双最适合自己。
> > *Jiù xiàng mǎi xiézi qián huì xiān guàng yíguàng yíyàng, kànkan nǎ yīshuāng zuì shìhé zìjǐ.*
> >
> > 我们会把不同的方案和选择都摆出来，让您挑到**最适合您的那一套**。
> > *Wǒmen huì bǎ bùtóng de fāng'àn hé xuǎnzé dōu bǎi chūlái, ràng nín tiāo dào zuì shìhé nín de nà yī tào.*
> 
> ---
> 
> **结尾问问题（预约）：**
> 
> > 这种咨询**面对面**会比较清楚，所以想请问一下，您是住在**哪一带**呢？
> > *Zhè zhǒng zīxún miàn duì miàn huì bǐjiào qīngchǔ, suǒyǐ xiǎng qǐngwèn yíxià, nín shì zhù zài nǎ yí dài ne?*
> 
> ---
> 
> 💡 *在最近的商场咖啡厅安排会面，并通过 WhatsApp 确认。*

### Callback Script — Consultant Follow-Up Call (Young Adults)

**Audience:** young-adult · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** callback, follow-up

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 (Kenny's Script)**

> Hi [Name], this is Kenny. My assistant may have reached out to you yesterday. This is just a quick 5 mins call for me to personally follow up with a few details from you to ensure this opportunity can truly be of value to you. This opportunity is specifically for individuals under the age of 25, so just to confirm, are you below 25?
> 
> ---
> 
> **After they confirm:**
> 
> That's great! Then this financial planning course would be incredibly useful for helping you take control of your finances and plan for the future. It's great that young people like you are starting to think about how they can better optimise their finances, and this is designed to guide you through that process.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Fact-finding / rapport building:**
> 
> To gain a better knowledge of where you are right now, may I ask when's your birthday?
> 
> Also, what milestones which are important are coming up for you? Like your ORD? Or graduation or even bigger plans for the future?
> 
> ---
> 
> **After they share milestones:**
> 
> Wow! That's exciting to hear and it's a great time to start thinking about how to plan financially for these milestones.
> 
> I'll send you a quick overview of what we'll be discussing via WhatsApp. Are you on WhatsApp now? Great, I've just sent it to you — can you check if you've received it?
> 
> Awesome! Could you also save my number so I can share additional resources with you later? Just scroll to the top and you should see a "Save Contact" button. Got it? Perfect!
> 
> ---
> 
> **Sharing the heart of the program:**
> 
> One of the reasons I wanted to reach out is that most people don't learn how to grow or invest their money while they're young. Schools often focus on helping us to study hard, find a job, but they don't teach us how to build wealth or create financial freedom for ourselves.
> 
> What I'll be sharing with you includes things like:
> 
> • How to kickstart your investing journey even with a small amount of money
> • Ways to manage and make your money grow over time
> • And how investing in yourself and financial knowledge can set you up for life
> 
> "The earlier you start, the bigger the impact — what you learn and do at 20, 25 can compound over decades. Waiting until you're older, like 40 or 50, just doesn't give you the same head start. Time is one of the most powerful multipliers, and starting early lets you take full advantage of it."
> 
> ---
> 
> **Pitching the Zoom consultation:**
> 
> Here's what I'd like to do: We'll have a quick Zoom call where I'll share a personalised consultation to help you understand your financial options and how you can start growing your money right now. This will be a chance to talk about things like:
> 
> • What goals do you have, whether it's saving for something big or just getting started with investing
> • The different options available to you for growing your money
> • And how to set yourself up financially for the future, even if you're just starting out
> 
> At the end of the consultation, I'll also give you free access to a course that covers personal finance, productivity, and self-improvement. It's an additional resource to help you build the right habits and mindset for long-term success.

**Version 2 (Full Callback Script — V2 (Under 30 + Course Pitch))**

> I believe my assistant gave you a call earlier. Alright, so just to clarify, this programme is actually only for people **below 30**. May I check — are you currently below 30?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Qualifying & Rapport Building
> 
> I see, that means you should be **[serving your National Service now / studying]**. Okay, great. In that case, I think this would actually be quite useful for you.
> 
> The reason is that we prefer to speak to young adults, as this programme is specifically designed to benefit people at your stage of life.
> 
> **[So you're currently in NS, which means your ORD should be around 2026 / So you're currently in school, which means your graduation should be around 2026/2027]**. Got it.
> 
> And you're 20 this year, right? Have you already turned 21? When's your birthday? Okay, understood.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### WhatsApp Resource Sharing
> 
> I'll be sending you some resources over WhatsApp. Are you on WhatsApp right now? Alright, I've just sent you a few links that you can take a look at.
> 
> I'll also be sending you more resources in the future. To make sure you receive them, just **save my number**. If you scroll to the top of WhatsApp, there should be a "Save Contact" button. Saving my number will allow me to share additional resources with you beyond this course.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Why We Created This Programme
> 
> Before I go into the course details, I want to briefly share why we created this programme.
> 
> The things we teach here aren't commonly taught in schools. Schools generally train people to become better employees or workers. What we aim to share are concepts that most young adults never get exposed to — things like **productivity, self-improvement, entrepreneurship, and investing in yourself**. These are incredibly important life skills.
> 
> Our team has been doing this for about **10 years now** — this is actually our 10th year — and our focus has shifted toward educating and coaching young adults. The reason is simple: **what you learn and practice when you're young creates a ripple effect throughout your entire life**. Something learned at 25 has far more impact than something learned at 65.
> 
> That's why this course was built. It's meant to be a **free, easy, self-study course** with no deadlines. Our hope is that it will genuinely be useful for you.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Pitching the Zoom Session
> 
> What I'll do next is walk you through the course in more detail over a **Zoom call**. After that, I'll give you **full access to the course at no cost**. Doing it over a Zoom call allows me to explain things clearly and tailor it better for you.
> 
> Would next **Saturday morning** work for you? Great. Let's do next **Saturday at 10am** over Zoom. It'll be a short session where I'll walk you through the course and give you access immediately afterward.
> 
> During the call, I'll also give you a **complimentary personal financial consultation**. This allows me to take a quick look at your situation and give you some personalised recommendations.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### Closing
> 
> If you have any friends who might be interested as well, feel free to share the links with them. They're welcome to join, and we can go through this together.
> 
> That's about it for now. I'll see you next Saturday at 10am on Zoom. Take care, and I'll speak to you soon. Bye-bye!

**Version 3 (Benjamin's Script — V3 (Structured + Referrals))**

> ### 1️⃣ Initial Introduction
> 
> Hi [Name], this is Benjamin. My assistant may have reached out yesterday. This is just a quick 5-min call in which I wanted to personally follow up with a few details and quick questions to ensure that this opportunity can truly be of value to you. This opportunity is specifically for individuals under the age of 25, so just to confirm, are you below 25?
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 2️⃣ Establishing Relevance
> 
> That's fantastic! Then this financial planning course would be incredibly useful for helping you take control of your finances and start planning for your future. It's great that young people like you are starting to think about how they can better optimise their finances (grow their money), and this is designed to guide you through that process.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 3️⃣ Rapport Building
> 
> To get a better sense of where you are right now, when's your birthday? Also, what milestones are coming up for you — like ORD, graduation, or even big plans for the future?
> 
> > *Use their response to add into your calendar and mark their current stage in the CRM.*
> 
> That's exciting! This is a great time to start thinking about how to plan financially for those milestones.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 4️⃣ WhatsApp + Save Number
> 
> I'll send you a quick overview of what we'll be discussing via WhatsApp. Are you on WhatsApp now? Great, I've just sent it to you — can you check if you've received it?
> 
> *(Wait for confirmation. Send them a quick reply using your WhatsApp Business quick replies.)*
> 
> Awesome! Could you also **save my number** so I can share additional resources with you later? Just scroll to the top of the message, and you should see a "Save Contact" button. Got it? Perfect!
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 5️⃣ Sharing the Heart of the Programme
> 
> **Why This Exists:**
> One of the reasons I wanted to reach out is that most people don't learn how to grow their money or invest while they're young. Schools often focus on helping us become good employees, but they don't teach us how to build wealth or create financial freedom for ourselves.
> 
> **Core Topics:**
> What I'll be sharing with you includes things like:
> 
> • How to get started with investing, even with a small amount of money
> • Ways to manage your money to make it grow over time
> • And how investing in yourself and your financial knowledge can set you up for life
> 
> **The Ripple Effect:**
> The earlier you start, the bigger the impact — what you learn and do at 20 or 25 can compound over decades. Waiting until you're older, like 40 or 50, just doesn't give you the same head start.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 6️⃣ Positioning the Consultation
> 
> **The Zoom Call:**
> Here's what I'd like to do: We'll have a quick Zoom call where I'll share a personalised consultation to help you understand your financial options and how you can start growing your money right now. This will be a chance to talk about things like:
> 
> • What goals you have, whether it's saving for something big or just getting started with investing
> • The different options available to you for growing your wealth
> • And how to set yourself up financially for the future, even if you're just starting out
> 
> **The Course as a Bonus:**
> At the end of the consultation, I'll also give you **free access** to a course that covers personal finance, productivity, and self-improvement. It's an additional resource to help you build the right habits and mindset for long-term success.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 7️⃣ Booking the Appointment
> 
> Let's set up a quick Zoom call — it'll only take about **30 minutes**, and I promise you'll walk away with a clear plan for your next steps. Does next **Saturday morning at 10am** work for you?
> 
> *(Wait for confirmation and adjust as needed.)*
> 
> Perfect! I'll send you the Zoom link closer to the date, and we'll go through everything step by step.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 8️⃣ Encouraging Referrals
> 
> If you know anyone else under 25 who might be interested in learning how to grow their money and invest, feel free to share this with them. Here's a quick script you can use:
> 
> > *"Hey, I came across this free financial consultation for young adults that teaches how to start investing and growing your money. It's super insightful, and you also get access to a free course afterward. Let me know if you're interested, and I'll share the details!"*
> 
> They're welcome to join the call with you or book their own time.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 9️⃣ Closing
> 
> To recap, during our Zoom call, we'll focus on understanding your financial goals, exploring different ways to grow your wealth, and building a personalised plan for you. And, as a bonus, you'll get access to a free course that dives deeper into these topics. Sound good?
> 
> Great! I'll see you next Saturday at 10am on Zoom. If you have any questions before then, just let me know. Take care, and I'll talk to you soon!

### Callback Call — Working Adult / Parent Consultation (Telemarketer Set)

**Audience:** working-adult · **Role:** telemarketer · **Tags:** callback, follow-up

**Version 1 (Consultant Callback — Parent Angle + Shoe Analogy)**

> ## 📞 Callback Script — Working Adult / Parent Consultation
> 
> *Use when the telemarketer has already set a callback appointment with a working adult / parent prospect*
> 
> ---
> 
> **Opening:**
> 
> > Hey, is this **[Name]**?
> >
> > Great. This is regarding the **retirement planning consultation**. I believe my assistant spoke with you yesterday to arrange this call. I tried giving you a ring earlier, but you might've been tied up, so let me just give a quick introduction.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Personal Introduction + Value Proposition:**
> 
> > I'm a retirement specialist, who is also a **mummy of two young kids**, and I work very closely with many parents to help them optimize their retirement setup — both on the **income side** and the **expense side**. For example:
> >
> > ✅ Creating additional streams of **passive income** to fund their retirement or **children's education**
> >
> > ✅ Reviewing existing **insurance or retirement policies**
> >
> > ✅ Minimizing **unnecessary costs** that tend to creep up as we age
> 
> ---
> 
> **Fact-Finding (Family):**
> 
> > Are you married? How many kids do you have now? How old are they?
> >
> > Have you started planning for their **education fund**?
> 
> ---
> 
> **Emotional Hook — Gift to Your Children:**
> 
> > Many people know that retirement planning is important, but what many do not know is that **proper retirement planning is actually the greatest gift you can give to your children** — it helps ease their burden in supporting your future expenses when they need money the most.
> >
> > Imagine **10–20 years down the road**, your children will be getting married / BTO / forming their own family. That's the period where they need money the most, and if you do proper planning now, we can help you create an **asset** that generates a sum of money for you to help them with their expenses — or help yourself to **retire early**.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Soft Close (Shoe Analogy):**
> 
> > At the very least, it's an **information-sharing session**. Worst-case, you walk away with the **NTUC voucher**. Best-case, you get insights that meaningfully improve your retirement strategy.
> >
> > Think of it like **window-shopping** before you buy a pair of shoes — you just want to explore options, compare, and see what fits you best.
> >
> > That's what this consultation is about: showing you the different **options and variations** so you can see what's most suitable for your situation.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Booking:**
> 
> > For this kind of conversation, it works best to **meet in person**. Which area do you stay around?
> 
> ---
> 
> 💡 *Set the meeting at the nearest mall café and confirm via WhatsApp.*

## appointment

### The ABCD Four Promises (Before Starting FHR)

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** fhr, rapport, trust-building, appointment

**Version 1 — The ABCD Four Promises – Pre-FHR Opening (AIA)**

> # The ABCD Four Promises
> 
> > *To be delivered before starting the Financial Health Review (FHR)*
> 
> ---
> 
> ## A — Assurance / Promise
> 
> This meeting is not to sell you anything. I am here to find out more about you and your goals. If we should proceed with proposing any solution, it is because I had shared something that is of value to you and that you felt comfortable with me.
> 
> ---
> 
> ## B — In Your Best Interest
> 
> In the event that I am able to proceed with the proposal stage, you can have my promise that what I plan for you will be exactly what I will do for myself if I'm in your shoes.
> 
> ---
> 
> ## C — Confidentiality Clause
> 
> I like to assure you that whatever we are going to share today will be kept in strictest confidence.
> 
> ---
> 
> ## D — Full Disclosure
> 
> With that in mind, I hope that you can provide me with as much information as you can, so I can make my best recommendation to meet your needs.

### Meeting Opening — Intent and Value Statement

**Audience:** general · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** opening, rapport, appointment, consultation

**Version 1 (Leo Tan)**

> Intent Statement
> If towards the end of the conversation, you feel that there’s a gap that you want to explore how to resolve then we can talk about the next possible steps in a second meeting. If not, just take today’s session as a session to understand your needs and to see how I can best assist you. 
> In the event that I can't assist you, I'll be very transparent to let you know. But if, let's say, there are ways in which I could potentially be of service to you, I'll also share that with you. Of course, at the end of the day, whether you decide to work with me is entirely your choice. So let's just take today's session as an exploratory session. The most important thing which we must achieve is to value add to you.

### Warm Market — Handling Ghosting & Non-Replies

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** texting-eq, follow-up, ghosting, warm-market

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 (Playful Bump)**

> ### When they ghost you:
> 
> > "Eh bro.. Don't ignore me leh HAHA don't like FA just say…"
> 
> Usually they reply: "Paiseh bro HAHA damn busy with work"
> 
> Then: "Me TOO" → schedule for a few weeks/months later when both are more free.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Key mindset:** Don't take it personally. They're busy, not rejecting you. Keep the tone light and humorous.

**Version 2 (Taking the Ball Back)**

> ### When they say "not now":
> 
> Prospect: "Not at the moment bro. Maybe when I am on long leave."
> 
> You: "Okay no worries! Check in with you around November!"
> 
> ---
> 
> **Key principle:** Always take the ball back. Make sure the right to reschedule remains with YOU, not the prospect. Set a specific follow-up month.

**Version 3 (Gentle Re-Engage)**

> ### Following up with someone who forgot to reply:
> 
> > "Hehe let's catch up and review soon!! Next sat/sun we go for a meal?"
> 
> Then later:
> > "OH YA — actually I just spent an hour studying your case just now 😂
> > And I wanna show you something my clients were sharing with me
> > Catch you before the month ends?"
> 
> ---
> 
> **Why it works:**
> - Opens with casual social catch-up (not financial planning)
> - Shows you've invested time in THEIR case
> - Creates curiosity without revealing everything

---

### Scheduled Follow-Up Texts — Young Adults (Every 2 Days)

**Audience:** young-adult · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** follow-up, text-sequence, text-message, reminder-sequence

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 (1st Follow-Up (Day 2))**

> Hey [Name]! Hope you're doing well. I understand things can get busy.
> 
> Just wanted to follow up on my previous message about helping young adults like yourself with their finances. I truly believe in the importance of financial literacy, especially for individuals in your age group.
> 
> I would love to meet with you on Zoom next week on **Saturday at 11am or 2pm** or **Sunday at 2pm** to touch on points like early retirement, savings, and investments. I'll pass you the free financial planning course during our short 20-minute consultation.
> 
> I'm confident you'll learn something new 😊

**Version 2 (2nd Follow-Up (Day 4))**

> Hey [Name]! Just wanted to reach out one more time to make sure you didn't miss my previous messages. I know life gets busy, but I'd love to connect and share some valuable financial insights that could really set you up for the future.
> 
> This isn't just about completing a financial planning/literacy course — during our **free 20-minute Zoom session**, I'll help you look at your financial goals and provide personalised advice that's relevant to your current situation. Many young adults and NSFs have found it to be eye-opening and really helpful, and I think you'd benefit too! 💪
> 
> Would you be available next **Saturday at either 10am or 2pm** or **Sunday at 2pm**? If another time works better, feel free to let me know!
> 
> Looking forward to the chance to chat and empower you with some great financial tools 😊

**Version 3 (3rd Follow-Up (Day 6 — Final))**

> Hi [Name], I hope you're doing well! 😊
> 
> Just wanted to follow up one last time about our free financial planning course and consultation. I completely understand if you've been busy, but I didn't want you to miss out on this opportunity.
> 
> This free Zoom session isn't just about finances — it's about helping young adults like you take control of your future. Whether it's learning how to save smarter, plan for early retirement, or start investing, we'll cover it all in just 20 minutes.
> 
> If you're interested, I have availability next **Saturday at 10am or 2pm** and **Sunday at 2pm**. If another time works better, let me know!
> 
> Looking forward to helping you kick-start your financial journey 💪
> 
> ---
> 
> *📝 After 2 follow-ups with no reply → mark lead as "Texting but no reply" in CRM.*

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 (NR 1 — First No-Reply Follow-Up)**

> ## 📲 No-Reply Follow-Up 1 — FB Webinar Leads
> 
> *Send if lead doesn't reply to the post-call text*
> 
> ---
> 
> > Hey, just bumping this up!
> >
> > People whom we are looking for are usually pretty motivated to get an **additional income stream**, **improve themselves**, or even **build a business**. We only have a few slots left as registration will be **closing this week**, so let me know if you would like to register. You may ask your friends to join with you as well.
> >
> > Do let me know **Thursday 5pm**, **Friday 2pm**, or **Saturday 10am** works for a Zoom call? It will take at most **30 minutes**.

**Version 2 (NR 2 — Second No-Reply Follow-Up)**

> ## 📲 No-Reply Follow-Up 2 — FB Webinar Leads
> 
> *Send if lead still doesn't reply after NR 1*
> 
> ---
> 
> > Just following up! Understand life can get busy, but I sincerely believe this may be a **life-changing opportunity**!
> >
> > Just letting you know that there will be around **3–4 rounds of interviews** after the session. Do let me know **Thursday 5pm**, **Friday 2pm**, or **Saturday 10am** works for a Zoom call? It will take at most **30 minutes**.
> >
> > If not, I can give you a **call tomorrow around 9–10pm** to do a quick intro.

**Version 3 (FAQ — "What's This About?")**

> 
> *Use when lead asks what the seminar/session is about*
> 
> ---
> 
> > Hey **[Name]**! Fair question 😊
> >
> > This is about a **free seminar** where we teach motivated people how to **build additional income streams**.
> >
> > And rather than motivating you or hyping you up, we want to also give the **"what"** and the **"how"** and help you do it with as little friction as possible. We also provide access to our **online learning platform**.
> >
> > The seminar is a **30-min Zoom call** where we'll cover:
> >
> > ✅ The opportunities of a **franchise business**
> >
> > ✅ **Success stories** → how I personally built a **7-figure business as a fresh graduate**
> >
> > ✅ How to **get started** even with **limited time/capital**
> >
> > **No obligation** — just valuable info you can use right away.
> >
> > Would **Thursday 5pm**, **Friday 2pm**, or **Saturday 10am** work for you?

### Consultant Text to Referee (Referral)

**Audience:** recruitment · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** recruitment, referral

**Version 1 (Consultant → Referee WhatsApp Text)**

> ## 📲 Consultant Text to Referee
> 
> ### Template Message
> 
> > Hey **[Name]**, got your contact from **[Referrer Name]**. It's about the online course that your friend shared with you about.
> >
> > It's self study, at your own pace. You can sign up here:
> > 🔗 https://www.skool.com/finternship/about

**Version 2 (Referral Model — Full Process Flow & SOP)**

> ## 📋 Referral Model for Recruitment + Sales — Process Flow
> 
> ### 🎯 Objective
> 
> Create an affiliate model for more **high-quality, organic referrals**, without needing too much time/effort on the consultant's part.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### ⚠️ Why Traditional Referrals Don't Work
> 
> - Getting people to refer friends is hard unless they **trust us for years**
> - It is **unethical to pay people** to meet us for an appointment directly, then try to sell them something
> - Common excuses: *"I'll check with them first"*, *"They already have an advisor"*, *"I'm not sure if they need this"*
> - These are excuses for **not wanting to look bad** in front of their friends
> - Forcing them to give contacts is difficult + **PDPA concerns**
> - Paying in cash/vouchers attracts the **wrong kind of people** and lowers our standing
> 
> ---
> 
> ### ✅ The Solution: Value Stack + "Good Lobang" Mentality
> 
> Use the **FINternship program** as a value stack and "Grand Slam Offer" to get referrals for both **sales and recruitment**.
> 
> Create a referral model where we **pay someone each time they sign up for the course**, which indirectly gives us recruitment leads and sales leads.
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 🔄 Process Flow to Get Referrals
> 
> 1. **After the appointment**, share that we have a **free course**
> 2. Briefly run through the program: https://tinyurl.com/FINternshipSG and the course page: https://www.skool.com/finternship/about
> 3. Share that they will get **$30 for each person who signs up**
> 4. "Successful sign-up" = They sign up on the Skool link **+ agree to have an appointment** with the consultant
> 5. Send the **Referral Template Script** for them to forward to friends
> 6. When there is a sign-up, it will show up on the **Skool membership requests page**
> 7. Check if they answered the membership question ("What is your contact number?")
> 8. If they didn't answer → ask the referrer for their contact (they will usually agree)
> 9. **Text the referee** to schedule a Zoom call **before unlocking** the course modules
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 💰 Rules & Incentives
> 
> | Role | Incentive |
> |---|---|
> | **Referrer** | $30 cash per successful sign-up + appointment |
> | **Consultant** | Pays $30 to referrer; leads are "consultant-generated leads" under Commission Structure |
> | **Leo** | Pays $30 ad spend to consultant if lead is referred for recruitment appointment (must be close to ORD/already ORD/existing students) |
> | **Recruitment Close** | If lead becomes an agent → parked under consultant; **$1,000 cash incentive** upon joining |
> 
> ---
> 
> ### 📝 Key Notes
> 
> - The consultant will fork **$30 cash** to pay the referrer
> - These leads are considered **"consultant generated leads"** under the Commission Structure for Sales
> - For recruitment leads, they need to be aware of the **FA job** and be close to ORD/already ORD/existing students
> - If recruited and becomes an agent → **parked under the consultant** if they decide to move up

**Version 3 (Leo Tan)**

> Hey XXX, got your contact from XXX. It’s about the online course that your friend shared with you about. It’s self study, at your own pace. You can sign up here https://www.skool.com/finternship/about
> To find out more: https://tinyurl.com/FINternshipSG.
> Can I schedule a quick zoom call with you to go through the overview and objectives of the courses and will give you access afterwards!

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 — Standard Template (Standard Template)**

> 
> Thank you for trusting us for your financial planning.
> 
> The endorsed copy of the policy documents has been emailed to you previously.
> 
> To view the policies online, please register an AIA account on our customer portal here: https://aiaplus.aia.com.sg/register
> 
> You can also download the "AIA+" app via phone to view your policies anytime.
> 
> As discussed during the appointment, we have analyzed the following details before we came out with our proposal!
> 
> 📌 Your finances, needs analysis
> 
> 📌 Current financial plans for consideration
> 
> 📌 Product Mechanics and Features, such as policy term, premiums, premium amount, freelook period, fees and charges
> 
> We hope that after going through the fact find and needs analysis, we have found something suitable for your needs, but as all policies are long term commitments with penalties for short term cancellations, please do let us if you have any concerns and you can change your mind within 14 days.
> 
> We also made a Portfolio Summary of your current policies:
> 
> [insert policy summary link by Aira in Policy Summaries Group Chat and you can find it on the CRM as well]
> 
> Also, you can refer to this basic financial planning guide by MAS as a useful resource: https://www.mas.gov.sg/regulation/guidance/basic-financial-planning-guide
> 
> Once again, thank you so much for entrusting us with your financial planning! We look forward to serving you to the best of our ability.
> 
> I would also recommend if we can do at least a yearly review around this time at least to run through all your policies, run through your financial planning and refresh your memory on what you have with AIA and other companies, shall we lock in a date around [insert a date 1 year from today then mark as an annual recurring event, and mark on your calendar to remind the lead again]

### Payment Methods — How to Ask Clients to Pay

**Audience:** clients · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** premium-payments, payment-methods, servicing, giro, ibanking, axs, payez, new-business, overdue, lapse, 30-day-overdue, urgent-reminder, premium-holiday, manual-payment

*5 versions:*

**Version 1 — Full Payment Guide (Payez / iBanking / AXS) (Admin)**

> Hey [name], You can make payment here:
> 
> **Option 1: Payez:**
> 
> https://myaia.aia.com.sg/en/myaia-payez/login.html
> 
> You can setup the recurring GIRO/Credit Card payments here and make one-time payment here above.
> 
> > Note: For recurring or one-time payment arrangement, VISA is not allowed for 2nd year onwards. Other methods include PayNow, Mastercard, GIRO (most recommended)
> 
> **Option 2: iBanking**
> 
> You can also make payment via iBanking:
> 
> Please follow the steps below:
> You can just pay for the plan using iBanking or AXS
> 
> These are the steps:
> 
> 1) Login to your iBanking phone app.
> 
> If you're using DBS, click here: https://internet-banking.dbs.com.sg/IB/Welcome
> 
> For OCBC: https://internet.ocbc.com/internet-banking/MobileApp/Index#login/login
> 
> For UOB: https://pib.uob.com.sg/PIBLogin/Public/processPreCapture.do?keyId=lpc
> 
> 2) Press 'Pay Bills' and go to 'One Time Payment'
> 
> 3) Under Billing organization, select 'AIA Singapore Private Limited'
> 
> 4) Your Reference Number is your Policy Number: [insert Policy Number]
> 
> 5) The amount to transfer is $[amount to pay]
> 
> **Option 3: AXS**
> 
> You can also make payment via AXS using the steps below:
> 
> https://e-station.axs.com.sg/
> 
> You can follow this for step by step!
> 
> 1) Click Bills
> 
> 2) Insurance
> 
> 3) Select AIA
> 
> 4) New application
> 
> 5) Insert your details
> 
> Policy number: [INSERT POLICY NUMBER]
> 
> Amount: [INSERT AMOUNT TO PAY]

**Version 2 — New Business — AXS & PayEZ (Admin)**

> ## 💼 For New Business Policies
> 
> Use this when the client's policy has just been issued and they need to make their first payment.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Option 1: Via AXS**
> 
> Hey [name],
> 
> I need your help to remit the amount of [amount] at your earliest convenience. If you have any questions or need assistance, please don't hesitate to reach out.
> 
> This letter is for your reference. [insert pending memo]
> 
> Thank you for your attention to this matter, and I appreciate your prompt action.
> 
> https://e-station.axs.com.sg/
> 
> You can follow this step by step!
> 
> 1) Bills
> 2) Insurance → AIA
> 3) New Application
> 4) Insert your details:
>    - Bill Reference: [INSERT POLICY NUMBER]
>    - Amount: [INSERT AMOUNT]
> 
> ---
> 
> **Option 2: Via PAY EZ**

> Hey [name],
> 
> Your policy has been issued. In order for it to be in-force, you may make payment through this link:
> 
> https://payez2.aia.com.sg/
> 
> This letter is for your reference. [insert pending memo]
> 
> You can click **"Make One-Time Payment"**.
> 
> Then, set up **recurring payments** for hassle-free payments subsequently.

**Version 3 — Overdue Policies — Reminder to Pay (Admin)**

> ## ⚠️ For Overdue Policies
> 
> Use this when a client's policy payment is overdue and needs a gentle nudge.
> 
> ---
> 
> Dear [Name],
> 
> I hope this message finds you well. I'm reaching out to kindly request your assistance in remitting the overdue amount of **[Amount]** for your policy at your earliest convenience.
> 
> To make the payment, please follow these steps:
> 
> 1) Visit: https://e-station.axs.com.sg/
> 2) Navigate to **Bills → Insurance → AIA**
> 3) Select **New Application**
> 4) Enter your details:
>    - Bill Reference: [INSERT POLICY NUMBER]
>    - Amount: [INSERT AMOUNT]
> 
> If you have any questions or require assistance, don't hesitate to reach out. I'm happy to help.
> 
> This letter is for your reference. [insert edoc memo of failed payments]
> 
> > 💡 **Tip:** The tone for overdue policies is more formal & empathetic compared to new business scripts. Always attach the edoc memo of failed payments for reference.

**Version 4 — Premium Overdue 30+ Days — Urgent Reminder (Leo)**

> ⚠️ **When to use:** Policy has been overdue for **30 days or more**. This is an urgent reminder with screenshot proof and step-by-step payment instructions.
> 
> ---
> 
> Hey [name]
> 
> I just want to inform you that your policy [policy number] is overdue. Here's a screenshot for your reference. Please make a payment as soon as possible. Thank you! [insert screenshot]
> 
> Please arrange payment at your earliest convenience to keep your coverage active.
> 
> **Option 1: AIA PayEZ**
> https://myaia.aia.com.sg/en/myaia-payez/login.html
> 
> **Option 2: AXS**
> https://e-station.axs.com.sg/
> 
> You can follow this for step by step:
> 1. Click **Bills**
> 2. **Insurance**
> 3. Select **AIA**
> 4. **New application**
> 5. Insert your details
>    - Policy number: [INSERT POLICY NUMBER]
>    - Amount: [INSERT AMOUNT TO PAY]
> 
> ---
> 
> 💡 **Tip:** Attach a screenshot of the overdue notice from the AIA portal so the client can see the urgency. Follow up within 3 days if no response.

**Version 5 — Premium Holiday — Resume Payment (Leo)**

> 🏖️ **When to use:** Client's policy is on **Premium Holiday** and you need them to resume payments to reactivate the policy.
> 
> ---
> 
> Hey [name],
> 
> Just checking if you're able to settle the unpaid premium so we can remove your policy [Policy No.] from Premium Holiday.
> 
> Please see the attached screenshot showing the month when payments stopped.
> 
> For this, you will need to make a manual payment.
> Please follow the steps below. Let me know if you have any questions.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Option 1: Using iBanking**
> 
> 1. Login to your iBanking phone app:
>    - **DBS:** https://internet-banking.dbs.com.sg/IB/Welcome
>    - **OCBC:** https://internet.ocbc.com/internet-banking/MobileApp/Index#login/login
>    - **UOB:** https://pib.uob.com.sg/PIBLogin/Public/processPreCapture.do?keyId=lpc
> 2. Press **'Pay Bills'** and go to **'One Time Payment'**
> 3. Under Billing organization, select **'AIA Singapore Private Limited'**
> 4. Your Reference Number is your **Policy Number**
> 5. Enter the **Amount**
> 
> ---
> 
> **Option 2: Using AXS**
> 
> https://e-station.axs.com.sg/
> 
> 1. Click **Bills**
> 2. **Insurance**
> 3. Select **AIA**
> 4. **New application**
> 5. Insert your details:
>    - Policy number: [INSERT POLICY NUMBER]
>    - Amount: [INSERT AMOUNT TO PAY]
> 
> ---
> 
> 💡 **Tip:** Attach a screenshot of the premium holiday status from the AIA portal so the client can see exactly which month payments stopped. Follow up if no response within 5 days.

### CPF SA Closure Texting Campaign

**Audience:** clients · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** texting-campaign, cpf, sa-closure, retirement-planning, whatsapp, platinum-wealth-venture, pwv, broadcast

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 — CPF SA Closure — Script for Clients (Send to Parents)**

> **Instructions:**  
> Please send to ALL clients — whether or not they are on broadcast. Send this text to all clients individually.
> 
> **Do NOT text the following clients:**
> 
> -   Zheng Xuan
>     
> -   Tong Wei
>     
> -   Zefan
>     
> -   Teo Jia Kai
>     
> -   Daphne Tee's children (Shi Wei? Shi Wen?)
>     
> -   Jun Hao
>     
> 
> ---
> 
> **Script:**
> 
> Hey \[name\], just wanted to share that there was a recent CPF SA closure for CPF members above 55. Your parents' retirement planning may be affected, as their excess SA funds will be moved to the OA once they have hit the Full Retirement Sum for their cohort, and interest rates on those funds will drop from 4.0% in the SA to 2.5% in OA. If they are not sure about how this change will affect their retirement, or have questions about CPF Life and retirement readiness, I'm happy to share more with them so they can make more informed and empowered decisions for their retirement.
> 
> In many ways, the quality of our parents' retirement also intertwines with our own financial well being, which is why I thought of sharing this with you. If they are open to learning more, let me know if I could give you or them a call!
> 
> ![image.png](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-images/1776686750654-7d2tgycvz9b.png)
> 
> &nbsp;

**Version 2 — PWV Last Push — Script for All Clients**

> **Instructions:**
> Send to ALL clients individually.
> Deadline: By next Monday.
> Note to VA: Please try to arrange a physical meeting with the client at their house with the parents. Check Cynthia's calendar if she is available.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Script:**
> 
> Hey [name], I normally don't do this, but I just had to share this before our best selling retirement dividend solution, Platinum Wealth Venture closes on 31st March, as the product will be nerfed from April onwards.
> 
> Can I ask you for a big favour, if you can check with your parents if they will be interested to hear more about it, because I believe this is going to be very beneficial for them.
> 
> It's a 5-year plan, generates approximately 7% dividends from year 1, and is capital guaranteed upon death. Since it's mostly invested in bonds, many of my savvy clients and their parents are using this as a way to diversify their portfolio into high yield fixed income, especially with the loss of interest from their CPF Funds because of the recent SA closure.
> 
> The following are the main features:
> 
> 1) 7-8% p.a Dividends
> 2) 100% of premiums invested fr Day 1
> 3) Up to 15% Welcome Bonus (1st 3 Years)
> 4) 7% Investment Bonus (8th Year)
> 5) Yearly Performance Bonus (from 8th Year)
> 6) Short Premium Term (5 Years), min S$18,000 a year.
> 7) Access cash anytime (from 8th Year onwards)
> 8) Capital Guaranteed upon Death
> 9) Option of Secondary Insured
> 
> Dividends is paid out quarterly this version of plan will be withdrawn on **31 March 2025**. I am enclosing the brochure here for your reading.
> 
> https://www.aia.com.sg/content/dam/sg-wise/en/docs/our-products/en/aia-platinum-wealth-venture.pdf

**Version 3 — Winnie Script — Formal PWV Introduction**

> **Instructions:**
> Formal version for clients who prefer a more professional tone.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Script:**
> 
> Dear Valued Client:
> 
> **Platinum Wealth Venture**
> 
> Regards to the recent news on CPF Special account closure, AIA has launched a plan primarily focusing on regular dividend payout since last year. In case this plan may interest you, the following are the main features:
> 
> 1) 7-8% p.a Dividends
> 2) 100% of premiums invested fr Day 1
> 3) Up to 15% Welcome Bonus (1st 3 Years)
> 4) 7% Investment Bonus (8th Year)
> 5) Yearly Performance Bonus (from 8th Year)
> 6) Short Premium Term (5 Years), min S$18,000 a year.
> 7) Access cash anytime (from 8th Year onwards)
> 8) Capital Guaranteed upon Death
> 9) Option of Secondary Insured
> 
> Dividends in so far has been declared quarterly since March 2024 and this version of plan will be withdrawn on **31 March 2025**. I am enclosing the brochure here for your reading.
> 
> https://www.aia.com.sg/content/dam/sg-wise/en/docs/our-products/en/aia-platinum-wealth-venture.pdf
> 
> Let me know if you are keen to discuss further.
> 
> Thanks for your kind attention.

### Client Tiering System SOP

**Audience:** clients · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** onboarding, client-tiering, sop, welcome-message, gold-member, platinum, silver, telegram, broadcast, whatsapp, newsletter, referral, retention, the-hive

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 — Welcome Message — The Hive by Leo Tan (consultant)**

> Hi [Client's Name],
> 
> Welcome to The Hive by Leo Tan 🐝
> I'm Leo, and I want to personally thank you for trusting me as your financial partner. You're now part of a tight-knit community that believes in long-term growth, clarity in decision-making, and putting your future first.
> 
> Here's something I've seen too often:
> Advisors win a client… and then relax.
> But in my world, that's when the real work begins.
> 
> You're not just a number or a file — you're family now.
> Depending on your current tier — Silver, Gold, or Platinum — you have access to a curated list of services and benefits designed to help you build, grow, and protect your wealth, every step of the way.
> 
> Let's get you set up:
> 
> ✅ Your Quick-Start Guide
> 
> **Explore Your Member Privileges**
> Tap on the menu above to check out everything you're entitled to — from exclusive consults to educational resources. Some perks are tier-dependent, so do note any actionables needed to maintain or level up your tier.
> 
> **SAVE MY NUMBER RIGHT NOW**
> 📲 That way, you'll receive broadcast updates directly from me via WhatsApp — no spam, just the good stuff. You'll also be added to Leo's Wealth Brief, my weekly WhatsApp newsletter packed with:
> - Market insights
> - Crucial financial news
> - Portfolio tips
> - Giveaways, webinars, and more.
> 
> **Join My Private Telegram Channel**
> This is where I share unfiltered thoughts on my personal stock picks, crypto moves, and broader market plays — only for clients who want to go a layer deeper.
> 
> **Book Your Complimentary Consults**
> Based on your tier, you and your loved ones are entitled to free sessions for Will-Writing, CPF & Retirement Planning, Tax Consultations, and more. These aren't just value-adds — they're protective pillars of your financial ecosystem.
> 
> **Visit & Share My Website**
> www.leotanadvisory.com (placeholder – insert your real site)
> Know someone who needs to get serious about their money? Share it with them. Helping others win financially is what this community is all about.
> 
> 🎁 Something's On Its Way…
> Keep an eye on your doorstep — your Client Onboarding Kit is en route.
> A little welcome gift from me to mark the start of something great.
> 
> You didn't just hire an advisor — you gained a partner for the long haul.
> I'm here to walk this path with you, every step of the way.
> 
> Talk soon,
> Leo Tan
> Client Advisor | Founder of The Hive

**Version 2 — Background & Training — Mindset Shift After Attaining a Client (consultant)**

> Hey, welcome back, team.
> 
> Let's talk about a really common trap that most advisors fall into — and if you're honest with yourself, you might have fallen into it too.
> 
> It goes like this:
> You hustle hard. You nurture the lead. You handle objections. You deliver the pitch. And finally — boom — the client says yes.
> 
> And then what do most advisors do?
> 
> They relax.
> They breathe out.
> They let go and enjoy the win of "attaining" the client.
> 
> Now don't get me wrong — celebrate the win. Absolutely. But here's the hard truth:
> 
> **The moment a client says yes is not the finish line. It's the starting line.**
> 
> Let that sink in.
> 
> The real relationship — the trust, the loyalty, the retention, the referrals — that gets built after the signature, not before.
> 
> 🧠 **Shift Your Mindset: From Hunter to Gardener**
> 
> - Before the sale, you're the hunter. After the sale, you become the gardener.
> - The hunter chases the win.
> - The gardener nurtures what they've built.
> 
> That's why great advisors never go missing after a deal closes. They double down. They show up even more. They create moments that make the client feel seen, heard, and valued.
> 
> Because guess what? People don't stay because you gave them a killer pitch.
> They stay because they feel like you care — and that you're still invested in their journey.
> 
> 💬 **What Should You Say to a New Client?**
> 
> "Hey [Client's Name],
> I just want to say thank you again for choosing me as your advisor. A lot of people think the relationship ends once the paperwork is signed — but for me, that's just where it begins.
> You're now officially part of my client family — and I'm going to make sure you feel the difference every step of the way."
> 
> That one paragraph already puts you in the top 5% of advisors. Why?
> Because you're not acting like the deal is done — you're treating the client like they just entered something valuable.
> 
> 🎯 **Immediate Gratification = Long-Term Stickiness**
> 
> Here's something that works wonders: give them a win right away.
> 
> That could be:
> - A welcome message with clear next steps.
> - A free resource or guide relevant to their goals.
> - An onboarding kit delivered to their doorstep.
> - A quick "here's what to expect" voice note that makes them feel like you've got a process.
> 
> Why? Because:
> People remember how you make them feel — especially in the first 7 days.
> You don't need to wow them with complexity. Just show them you care. Be organized. Be proactive. Be human.
> 
> 🔁 **Recap – 3 Rules for Client Onboarding Success**
> 
> 1. **Don't disappear after the "yes."** Show up even stronger. Be present, be personal.
> 2. **Give immediate value.** The faster you deliver something meaningful, the stronger the bond becomes.
> 3. **Frame the relationship as a long-term partnership.** Help them see that they didn't just get a service — they joined a system, a community, a journey.
> 
> 🧭 **Final Thought**
> 
> In sales, a lot of people chase volume.
> But the best in the game? They master depth.
> 
> Depth builds trust. Trust builds loyalty. Loyalty builds your business.
> 
> So go back to your current clients. Ask yourself:
> "Who did I close… and then accidentally forget to follow up with?"
> 
> Re-engage them. Make them feel valued again.
> 
> Because in this business, it's not just about acquiring clients —
> It's about keeping them, serving them, and turning them into raving fans.
> 
> You got this.

**Version 3 — GOLD Member Congratulations Card (consultant)**

> ![image.png](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-images/1776686662175-744w4jkgi85.png)
> 
> 🥇 Congratulations – You're Now a GOLD Member!
> 
> Hi \[Client's Name\],
> 
> Welcome to the GOLD tier of our community — and congrats on unlocking a new level of access, support, and recognition.
> 
> You're now part of an exclusive group of clients who don't just get advisory services — you get the full experience of being valued, celebrated, and supported on your financial journey.
> 
> Here's what being a GOLD Member gives you:
> 
> 🌟 **Your Exclusive GOLD Privileges**
> 
> ✅ Invitations to Prestige Events & Seminars  
> Both recreational and financial — carefully curated for learning, connection, and fun.
> 
> ✅ 50% Discounted Wills  
> For you and your family, because peace of mind shouldn't be expensive.
> 
> ✅ Birthday F&B Gift Voucher  
> Your special day deserves a treat — it's our way of saying thank you.
> 
> ✅ Access to the Biweekly Ascendance Newsletter  
> Get handpicked tips and strategies for leveling up your personal and professional life — all in one digestible email every two weeks.
> 
> 📈 **Want to Unlock Platinum Status?**
> 
> Here's how you can level up:
> 
> -   **Refer 3 People From Your Network** — Help friends win financially — and help yourself free up time and resources.
>     
> -   **Grow Your Investments with Leo** — Let's review your Assets Under Management (AUM) and see what's possible. (Just reach out to check the benchmark!)
>     
> -   **Optimize Your Insurance Portfolio** — Let's reduce your premiums and increase your coverage with smarter structuring.
>     
> 
> 🔁 **Quick Reminders**
> 
> -   Your benefits are claimable yearly.
>     
> -   Membership tiers are reviewed and renewed every January.
>     
> 
> Let's make this GOLD year count.  
> Thank you for being a part of the journey — you're not just a client, you're family now.
> 
> Leo Tan  
> Your Partner in Growth | The Hive

### Coupon Payout Text

**Audience:** clients · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** premium-payments, coupon, servicing, smart-rewards-saver, payout

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 — Coupon Accumulated (Coupon Accumulated)**

> Hey \[name\]
> 
> I want to inform you that there is a $500.00 coupon payout from the plan, but we have already accumulated this into the coupon accumulation account for higher interest. You will receive this every year as your plan is a flexible plan and gives you the option to withdraw the amount.
> 
> Since you already chose option 2, we can still explore options 1 and option 3.
> 
> 1.  You can withdraw this $500.00 whenever you want if you need the cash.
>     
> 2.  The second option is just to ignore it. It will be accumulated back into a savings plan to accumulate a 2.75% interest rate.
>     
> 3.  The third option is that you can STILL withdraw this amount to create an essential insurance portfolio that will consist of a comprehensive accident and hospital plan or critical illness plan.
>     
> 
> You can also invest this $500.00 as well into a pure high-risk investment plan!
> 
> There are many ways to maximize it actually!
> 
> (insert screenshot)
> 
> Here is the letter for your reference.
> 
> Let me know if you're free this or next week to have a catch-up and review options 1 and 3 and their pros and cons.

> Hey [name]
> 
> I want to inform you that there is a $500.00 coupon payout from the plan, and its currently sent to your bank account. You will receive this every year as your plan is a flexible plan and gives you the option to withdraw the amount.
> 
> You are on option 1, but we can still explore options 2 and option 3.
> 
> You can withdraw this $500.00 whenever you want if you need the cash.
> 
> The second option is just to ignore it. It will be accumulated back into a savings plan to accumulate a 2.75% interest rate.
> 
> The third option is that you can STILL withdraw this amount to create an essential insurance portfolio that will consist of a comprehensive accident and hospital plan or critical illness plan.
> 
> You can also invest this $500.00 as well into a pure high-risk investment plan!
> 
> There are many ways to maximize it actually!
> 
> (insert screenshot)
> 
> Here is the letter for your reference.
> 
> Let me know if you're free this or next week to have a catch-up and review options 2 and 3 and their pros and cons.

### HSG Changes Client-Reactivation Funnel

**Audience:** clients · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** hospital-plan, reactivation, funnel, moh-updates, call-script, va-workflow, appointment-setting, client-review, hsg-changes, multi-step

*2 versions:*

**Version 1 — Step 1 — Cold Call Script (VA to Client)**

> [video](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-videos/1775542887545-qnz9cksxcpg.mp4)
> 
> Hey \[Name\]! This is \[Your Name\], calling on behalf of Leo, your AIA consultant.
> 
> This will just be a short call. Leo wanted to reach out to you because there have been some updates regarding hospital plans just recently.
> 
> He might have texted you previously, but you were probably busy, so I'm just calling to inform you of the changes.
> 
> Is it okay if we can do a zoom call or meetup to go through this? \[perhaps sometime this weekend morning/afternoon for you?\]
> 
> Okay great!
> 
> \[try to set appointment, do check Leo's calendar and try to tentatively set a timing, then just keep calling/reminding them to go through and to meet me physically or over a zoom call. If they really cannot, then just schedule for me a callback, and set a time on my calendar\]
> 
> ---
> 
> **Notes:**
> 
> **Who is Leo:** Leo is your AIA consultant. You may have purchased policies from AIA a while back.
> 
> You can briefly try to emphasize how important it is to know the changes, and also to review their policies, how it affects them etc (assume that they have policies from other agents/other companies).
> 
> **If they decline:**
> 
> 1.  Set a next day event for Leo to text: "Choo Shih Pang 82595900 - hey \[name\], my assistant called you yesterday regarding the hospital plan changes, let me know if we can arrange a review soon! If not I will text you again in 3 months time?"
>     
> 2.  Set a 3 months time event: "Choo Shih Pang 82595900 - Hey \[name\], it's been a while, will be good if we can arrange a yearly review soon, sometime this week or next better for you? Suggest 3 timings" — set a yearly event on my calendar.
>     
> 
> **If they said they already know:** Say that it may still be better for Leo to explain to you quickly on all of this.
> 
> **Focus on:** Financial simulator, financial projection, consolidation — you might have policies from different companies.
> 
> **CRM to check existing policies:** [https://nsgukkz32942.sg.larksuite.com/share/base/view/shrlgDliW7p26UUqCJYjM6XXgkf](https://nsgukkz32942.sg.larksuite.com/share/base/view/shrlgDliW7p26UUqCJYjM6XXgkf)
> 
> ---
> 
> **Conversion note:** Out of 100 calls, ~50% will decline → very important to follow the reactivation funnel.  
>   
> 
> 📎 [change-update-of-signature.pdf](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-resources/1775566017881-3ssjltvr2hb.pdf)

**Version 2 — Step 2 — Post-Appointment Group Chat Setup (Step 2 — Post-Appointment Group Chat Setup)**

> Once a meeting is set tentatively, please create a group chat with my number 89714581:
> 
> ---
> 
> Hey \[name\], great speaking to you just now, as confirmed, Leo will be doing a zoom call/physical meeting with you to update you on some of the MOH updates of hospital policies, as well as a review of your policies.
> 
> I've marked my calendar for our meeting as per the following:
> 
> 📅 Date/Time: \[Next Saturday 10am\]  
> 📍 Location: \[CWP\]  
> ⏱️ Duration: 30-60min
> 
> We'll be going through a portfolio update, some recommendations and will send you a financial report after the session.
> 
> ![image.png](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-images/1776684627189-7kx7s91cv4q.png)
> 
> I've reserved the slot on my calendar, do kindly reply this message "ok" to acknowledge.
> 
> Looking forward to our chat!
> 
> ---
> 
> **Group chat name:** "AIA Leo Medical Plan Changes with \[client name\]"
> 
> Keep reminding/calling to confirm/firm up the zoom call/meetup.

### Hospital Plan Rider Changes Text

**Audience:** clients · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** texting-campaign, hospital-plan, ip-rider, moh-changes, vitalhealth, healthshield, medical-insurance, whatsapp, retirement-planning, cross-sell, referral-ask, callback, post-meeting

*7 versions:*

**Version 1 — General Approach — MOH IP Rider Changes Announcement**

> Hi XXX
> 
> You should have heard by now the major changes to IP Plans announced by MOH today. This has a great impact on healthcare costs, your healthcare insurance planning and retirement planning.
> 
> In summary, new IP riders applied from 27th Nov would
> 
> No longer cover the minimum IP deductible amount (up to $3,500) of IP plans moving forward; and
> 
> The minimum co-payment cap per policy year will increase from $3,000 to S$6,000.
> 
> For existing IP Riders like Vital Health A, you will continue to enjoy the current benefits under your existing AIA Max VitalHealth* rider. AIA will monitor and review the claims experience from time to time, and should there be a need to revise existing features, please be assured that we will inform you on any changes affecting your coverage at least 31 days prior to the change taking effect.
> 
> I had attached 2 links below with more info. I am available for a phone call / zoom call to discuss these changes.
> 
> https://cna.asia/4rBgIXE
> https://www.aia.com.sg/en/our-products/health/medical-insurance/aia-healthshield-gold-max/moh-updates-ip-riders
> 
> 📺 Overview video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=I4RpUf2rP18

**Version 2 — If Client Has a Rider (VitalHealth A or B)**

> Hey \[name\]
> 
> Just wanted to let you know of the recent CNA article: [https://cna.asia/4rBgIXE](https://cna.asia/4rBgIXE).
> 
> In a nutshell, after April 2028, all hospital plans and their riders will no longer cover the deductible of $3.5k, and the co-payment limit will be raised to $6,000 per year.
> 
> &nbsp;
> 
> ![image.png](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-images/1776685225302-grvltyztgve.png)![image.png](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-images/1776685235344-4pvg3xwxdfs.png)
> 
> Since your plan and rider was purchased before **27th November,** you will still be able to enjoy the current benefits with your rider (deductible is covered, and co-payment limit is $3k per year), for now!
> 
> If your parents have any hospital plans, it would be good to forward the news to them, and I would be happy to explain further if they have any questions about their existing hospital coverage, as the increasing cost of these premiums might especially be a cause for concern in their retirement planning, and answer any questions about whether to maintain the rider/drop the rider.

**Version 3 — If Client Has No Hospital Plan or No Rider**

> **If they don't have Hospital Plan or No Rider**  
> 
> Hey \[name\]
> 
> Just wanted to let you know of the recent CNA article: [https://cna.asia/4rBgIXE](https://cna.asia/4rBgIXE).
> 
> In a nutshell, after April 2028, all hospital plans and their riders will no longer cover the deductible of $3.5k, and the co-payment limit will be raised to $6,000 per year.
> 
> &nbsp;
> 
> ![image.png](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-images/1776685394239-1kljzru6pme.png)![image.png](https://hgdbflprrficdoyxmdxe.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/knowledge-files/editor-images/1776685431176-uobkddpjtad.png)
> 
> I noticed that you don't have a hospital rider plan done up yet, so it might be a good time to review it to purchase it before April 2026, as you can still enjoy the coverage from the deductible waiver and co-payment cap of $6k before April 2028, if you purchase before March 31st 2026.
> 
> If your parents have any hospital plans, it would be good to forward the news to them, and I would be happy to explain further if they have any questions about their existing hospital coverage, as the increasing cost of these premiums might especially be a cause for concern in their retirement planning, and answer any questions about whether to maintain the rider/drop the rider.

**Version 4 — Post Meeting Follow-Up Text**

> Hey [name]! Good chat just now!
> 
> So as mentioned just now, depending on what plan your parents are on, from now until age 85, they may have to set aside around $200-$300k for their hospital plans.
> 
> Instead of wasting this $200-$300k to be paid to the insurer as an expense for the hospitalisation plan, we can use these resources to
> 
> • generate income to offset the hospitalisation plan premiums
> • Leave behind more money for your parents and yourself if need be
> 
> We can arrange a session with you and your parents to run through
> 
> • Share updates on the new hospital changes
> • Share about the impact of the hospital plan premiums on retirement
> • Share strategies to offset all this increasing costs with better retirement planning
> 
> Tentatively next Sunday 4-6pm with your parents to go through.
> 
> (insert image)
> 
> The above screenshot is for an example client at 57 years old, who has to pay more than $300k for his NTUC Income Shield plan.
> But, if he can do something, he can use the same money, and generate dividend income streams to pay off the hospital plan with excess left over, and still keeping the $300k+
> 
> Let me know if this or next weekend is a good time to share more with your parents.

**Version 5 — Callback Text — Version A (Formal)**

> Hey [name],
> 
> Called you recently, actually just wanted to let you know of the recent changes in the hospital plans.
> 
> In a nutshell, after April 2028, all hospital plans' coverage will be nerfed.
> 
> AIA should be launching a new hospital plan around in April, so it will actually be a good time for us to meet up to review your coverage and see if the new hospital plan is worth changing to (it's cheaper, but we can weigh the downsides to it).
> 
> It might also be helpful to check in with your parents about their medical coverage too!
> 
> Let me know if you'd like to have a 1) quick call or 2) text you again around in April to schedule a meeting?

## objection-handling

### Texting EQ — 4-Step Objection Handling Framework

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** texting-eq, objection-handling, framework, warm-market

**Version 1 (Texting EQ Guide)**

> ### 4-Step Objection Handling Framework
> *From Texting EQ by Wilfred Wong*
> 
> 90% of approaches are met with an objection. An objection is NOT a rejection.
> 
> ---
> 
> **Common objections you'll hear:**
> 1. Thanks bro, but I'm not interested
> 2. Cool! If I need it, I'll let you know
> 3. Oh thank you, I have an advisor already
> 4. It's ok, thank you!
> 5. I'm good, thank you!
> 6. I just did a review with my advisor
> 7. I am not looking to invest at this point
> 8. I am not looking to get any products now
> 9. I am getting married / buying house, bad time
> 
> ---
> 
> ### The 4 Steps:
> 
> **Step 1 — Casually Acknowledge**
> > "Hahaha YES I expected you to be not interested — if you're interested you would have already texted me 😂"
> 
> **Step 2 — Common Ground**
> > "Actually most of my clients also met me without feeling interested because they didn't know how I plan prior to meeting me."
> 
> **Step 3 — Different Perspective**
> > "So I want to show you how I improve my clients' investment returns to complement what you have."
> 
> **Step 4 — Make Them Feel Safe (Safety Valve)**
> > "Then from there you can decide for yourself if it's relevant to you? Sometimes the conclusion is to keep your portfolio unchanged also."
> 
> ---
> 
> **Then add:**
> - 🔑 An easy-to-answer question: "Which days are you usually in office?"
> - 💬 A personal element: "I haven't seen you in so long anyway / It'll be fun!"

---

### "Already Have an Advisor" — Objection Script

**Audience:** warm-market · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** texting-eq, objection-handling, warm-market, existing-advisor

**Version 1 (Case Study — Aunt as Advisor)**

> ### Objection: "My aunt is my advisor, and I don't mix friends with financial matters"
> 
> **Prospect:**
> > "My advisor is my aunt so I usually go to her. I have bad experiences with friends as advisors so I tend to stay away. Just being honest haha. If I ever need to approach someone else, I'll let you know."
> 
> **Response (Acknowledge → Common Ground → Different Perspective → Safety Valve):**
> 
> > "Something that my clients feedbacked to me was how they wanted an advisor that'll be there for them for the next 4-5 decades in the same life stage, while an older advisor can be a concurrent mentor at the same time."
> >
> > "Anyway, I'm really hoping we can have a super casual chat about this, so that moving forward I can communicate and advise my friends better in such a situation."
> 
> ---
> 
> **Result:** Prospect replied "lemme give it some thought" — door stayed open.
> 
> **Key takeaway:** You can have TWO advisors. Position the younger advisor as relatable, the existing one as a mentor. Don't try to replace — complement.

---

### Objection Handling -- Young Adults (All Objections)

**Audience:** young-adult · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** objection-handling, comprehensive

*7 versions:*

**Version 1 (MoneyBees Script — V1 (Ad Response))**

> **Q: Where did you get my number?**
> 
> **A:** You did an ad on Instagram a while back.
> 
> > *Don't say what company you're from.*

**Version 2 (MoneyBees Script — V2 (Telemarketer Angle))**

> **Q: How did you get my number?**
> 
> **A:** I am just a telemarketer for my manager. My manager reaches out to many people and some find his services very helpful and want to refer some of their friends to speak to us. I believe you are one of the contacts that he received, so I am calling in to check whether you would be keen to have a session with my manager.
> 
> *If they press further:*
> 
> I do not have access to the entire database so I am not too sure about that — maybe you can ask my manager when he contacts you.

**Version 3 (Team Objection Scripts)**

> ## 1. "Not interested"
> 
> I understand you may have some concerns. However, my main intention here is to just to help you get a financial headstart.
> 
> Don't worry, it is just a short zoom session to help you understand more about financial literacy concepts and it is just a 20 mins session out of your 24 hours a day. So would [date and time] work?
> 
> ## 2. "Is this compulsory?"
> 
> No, however most ORD Personnel and Students who have already attended this session found it to be very beneficial right after they have attended. So it will be beneficial for you to attend now that you have finished NS. We can set a tentative date on say [date and time]. Which is more convenient for you? Do try to attend it as well as time slots are specially allocated for you.
> 
> ## 3. "What is the session about?"
> 
> This would be a session to learn more ways you can grow your money faster, so that you can gain a headstart in your career compared to your peers.
> 
> So would [date and time] work better for you?
> 
> ## 4. "Is this a sharing session? Or a 1:1 session? Do you have any link?"
> 
> Yes this will be a group session whereby it will be quite an interactive session to go through some of the potential career options for you, of which many other ORD Personnel or Students have found it to be really beneficial for them. So would [date and time] work better for you?
> 
> ## 5. "How did you get my number?"
> 
> You actually signed up for an interest form about 2 years back, either during your enlistment or nearing your ORD. Do you still recall?
> 
> *If they say No:* Oh, many of the other guys do actually remember signing up for this, but no worries haha *(then go back to setting a time)*
> 
> ## 6. "Which organisation are you calling from?"
> 
> I'm actually calling from AIA, it's one of the largest financial institutions in Singapore and Asia, where we have been in the financial services industry for almost 100 years.
> 
> ## 7. "What is your name, want to complain"
> 
> *(At most give your first name — do NOT give your contact or mobile number)*
> 
> Hi Joshua XXX, I understand your frustration however this is just a service call to check if you are keen to understand how to better explore your internship and career options. I apologise for the inconvenience caused, if any.
> 
> **Then HANG UP** — the more you talk the more they will try to find trouble.

**Version 4 (MoneyBees Script)**

> **Q: I'm not interested.**
> 
> **A:** That's the case with most people even before I call. Many NSFs we work with also feel the same way at first. But when they meet us they find our ideas very useful. Just so that you can give yourself a chance to decide — would tomorrow morning OR afternoon be better to meet you?

**Version 5 (MoneyBees Script)**

> **Q: Is this compulsory?**
> 
> **A:** You can opt out.
> 
> > *If not keen, just end the call politely. If no objection, continue with your script as normal — "Where do you stay?" etc.*

**Version 6 (MoneyBees Script)**

> **Q: I'm not sure if I'm free on that day. Can you send me more details and I get back to you once I confirm my schedule?**
> 
> **A:** No worries. I'll send you some info through WhatsApp after this call, and you just help me to confirm the timing again okay?
> 
> *PAUSE.*
> 
> So one last thing… this session is just for you to learn more, and I'm just doing my job…
> 
> > *Continue with the rest of your script.*

**Version 7 (MoneyBees Script)**

> **Q: Can you send me more details first?**
> 
> **A:** No worries. I'll send you some info through WhatsApp after this call. And you just help me to reply the WhatsApp to confirm, okay?
> 
> Alright, so one last thing… this session is only for NSFs…
> 
> > *Continue with the rest of your script.*

---

### Objection Handling -- NSF (All Objections)

**Audience:** nsf · **Role:** consultant · **Tags:** objection-handling, nsf, comprehensive

*3 versions:*

**Version 1 (MoneyBees Script)**

> **Q: If prospect is hesitant…**
> 
> **A:** My manager has worked with many clients over the years and a lot of them found this sharing very helpful. There are no obligations, **BUT**, to set aside just 30 mins of your time. You can always walk away from the session at any time if you don't think it is suitable for you.
> 
> The takeaway is that as long as you can learn something beneficial from this session, then that is good enough for me. Is that fine?

**Version 2 (MoneyBees Script)**

> **Q: I'm not free on that day.**
> 
> **A:** No worries. I'll just put the slot 2 weeks from now then. I'll send you a WhatsApp to confirm the timing again, okay?
> 
> Alright, so one last thing… this session is only for NSFs.
> 
> > *Continue with the rest of your script.*

**Version 3 (MoneyBees Script)**

> **Q: I already have an agent / investment plan.**
> 
> **A:** Would you like to have an alternative opinion? The reason why I ask is because many of our clients also have more than one financial agent — this allows them to have multiple opinions as well as giving them different perspectives of their financial situation.
> 
> Furthermore, this will only take 30 mins of your time, and it's a free learning opportunity for yourself. You can walk away anytime with no obligation if you don't think the session helps you.
> 
> The takeaway is that as long as you can learn something beneficial from this session, then that is good enough for me. Will that be okay?
